          <h3 className="text-xl font-semibold text-gray-700 mb-2">
            暂无学科
          </h3>
          <p className="text-gray-500 mb-6">
            {phaseConfig[selectedPhase].label}学段尚未添加任何学科
          </p>
          {/* 教研员身份下隐藏添加按钮 */}
          {!isTeacher && (
            <button
              onClick={() => setShowAddSubjectDialog(true)}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-6 py-3 bg-emerald-600 text-white rounded-lg hover:bg-emerald-700 transition-colors text-sm font-medium"
            >
              <Plus className="w-4 h-4" />
              添加第一个学科
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      ) : (
        // 学科卡片网格
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
          {mockSubjects[selectedPhase].map((subject) => (
            <div
              key={subject.id}
              className="relative bg-white border-2 border-gray-200 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden hover:shadow-xl hover:border-emerald-400 hover:-translate-y-1 transition-all duration-300 cursor-pointer group"
            >
              <>
                <div className="p-6">
                  {/* 发布状态标记 */}
                  {(() => {
                    const stateKey = `${selectedPhase}-${subject.id}`;
                    const state = mockKnowledgeTreePublishState[stateKey];
                    if (state?.status === 'pending') {
                      return (
                        <div className="absolute top-3 right-3">
                          <span className="px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-amber-700 bg-amber-100 rounded-full shadow-sm">
                            待发布 · {state.pendingChanges}项变更
                          </span>
                        </div>
                      );
                    } else if (state?.status === 'published') {
                      return (
                        <div className="absolute top-3 right-3">
                          <span className="px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-emerald-700 bg-emerald-100 rounded-full shadow-sm">
                            已发布
                          </span>
                        </div>
                      );
                    }
                    return null;
                  })()}

                  {/* 学科图标和名称 */}
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-4 mb-5">
                    <div className={`w-14 h-14 ${subject.color.replace('bg-', 'bg-opacity-10 ')} rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center group-hover:scale-110 transition-transform duration-300`}>
                      <div className={`p-3 ${subject.color} rounded-lg text-white`}>
                        {subject.icon}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex-1">
                      <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-900 mb-1 group-hover:text-emerald-600 transition-colors">
                        {subject.name}
                      </h3>
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 font-medium">
                          {phaseConfig[selectedPhase].label}
                        </p>
                        {(() => {
                          const stateKey = `${selectedPhase}-${subject.id}`;
                          const state = mockKnowledgeTreePublishState[stateKey];
                          return state?.currentVersion && (
                            <span className="text-xs text-gray-500">
                              · {state.currentVersion}
                            </span>
                          );
                        })()}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* 数据统计 */}
                  <div className="space-y-3 mb-5">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-sm">
                      <span className="text-gray-500 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                        <BookOpen className="w-4 h-4" />
                        知识点总数
                      </span>
                      <span className="font-bold text-lg text-gray-900">
                        {subject.knowledgeCount}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-sm">
                      <span className="text-gray-500 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                        <Clock className="w-4 h-4" />
                        最近更新
                      </span>
                      <span className="text-gray-600 font-medium">
                        {subject.lastUpdateTime}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* 进入管理按钮 */}
                  <button
                    onClick={() => setSelectedSubject(subject)}
                    className="w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-4 py-3 bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-500 to-emerald-600 text-white rounded-xl hover:from-emerald-600 hover:to-emerald-700 transition-all duration-300 font-medium shadow-sm hover:shadow-md"
                  >
                    进入管理
                    <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4 group-hover:translate-x-1 transition-transform" />
                  </button>
                </div>

                {/* 装饰背景 */}
                <div className={`absolute top-0 right-0 w-32 h-32 ${subject.color} opacity-5 rounded-full -translate-y-16 translate-x-16`}></div>
              </>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );

  const KnowledgeTreeManagement = () => (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      {/* 状态条 - 显示发布状态 */}
      {pageState && (
        <StatusBar
          pageState={pageState}
          onPublish={() => setShowPublishModal(true)}
          onShowHistory={() => setShowHistoryModal(true)}
          onCancelScheduled={handleCancelScheduled}
          isTeacher={isTeacher}
        />
      )}
      
      {/* 顶部横跨式工具栏 */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm border border-gray-200 p-4">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
          {/* 左侧：返回按钮 + 搜索框 */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-4 flex-1">
            <button
              onClick={() => {
                setSelectedSubject(null);
                setIsEditingTree(false);
              }}
              className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 text-gray-700 rounded-lg hover:bg-gray-50 transition-colors text-sm"
            >
              <ArrowLeft className="w-4 h-4" />
              返回学科列表
            </button>
            
            {/* 搜索框 */}
            <div className="flex-1 max-w-md relative">
              <Search className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 transform -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 text-gray-400" />
              <input
                type="text"
                placeholder="搜索知识点..."
                value={searchKeyword}
                onChange={(e) => setSearchKeyword(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500 focus:border-emerald-500"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* 操作按钮 */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            {isEditingTree ? (
              <>
                <button
                  onClick={() => {
                    // 关键：先同步 contentEditable 编辑器的最新内容到 editData
                    const editorEl = document.querySelector('[contenteditable="true"]');
                    if (editorEl) {
                      const html = editorEl.innerHTML;
                      const tempDiv = document.createElement('div');
                      tempDiv.innerHTML = html;
                      const images: TextImage[] = [];
                      tempDiv.querySelectorAll('img').forEach((img, idx) => {
                        images.push({
                          id: `img-${idx}`,
                          url: img.src,
                          alt: img.alt || ''
                        });
                      });
                      
                      // 更新 editData 中的文字讲解内容
                      editData.textContent = {
                        content: html,
                        wordCount: tempDiv.textContent?.length || 0,
                        lastEditTime: new Date().toLocaleString(),
                        images
                      };
                    }
                    
                    // 完成知识树编辑，触发待发布状态
                    setIsEditingTree(false);
                    // 检测修改了哪些维度，生成对应的变更记录
                    if (pageState && selectedNode) {
                      const newChanges: ChangeRecord[] = [];
                      
                      // 检测知识点信息是否修改
                      const infoChanged = 
                        (editData.examFrequency !== undefined && editData.examFrequency !== selectedNode.examFrequency) ||
                        (editData.strategyType !== undefined && editData.strategyType !== selectedNode.strategyType) ||
                        (editData.personalizedStrategies !== undefined && 
                          JSON.stringify(editData.personalizedStrategies) !== JSON.stringify(selectedNode.personalizedStrategies));
                      
                      if (infoChanged) {
                        newChanges.push({
                          type: 'modify',
                          target: selectedNode.name,
                          detail: '修改知识点信息',
                          dimension: '知识点信息',
                          changedByRole: isTeacher ? 'teacher' : 'supervisor',
                          changedByAccount: String(Math.floor(100000 + Math.random() * 900000)),
                        });
                      }
                      
                      // 检测知识点结构是否修改
                      const structureChanged =
                        (editData.prerequisiteKnowledge !== undefined &&
                          JSON.stringify(editData.prerequisiteKnowledge) !== JSON.stringify(selectedNode.prerequisiteKnowledge)) ||
                        (editData.extensionKnowledge !== undefined &&
                          JSON.stringify(editData.extensionKnowledge) !== JSON.stringify(selectedNode.extensionKnowledge));
                      
                      if (structureChanged) {
                        newChanges.push({
                          type: 'modify',
                          target: selectedNode.name,
                          detail: '修改知识点结构',
                          dimension: '知识点结构',
                          changedByRole: isTeacher ? 'teacher' : 'supervisor',
                          changedByAccount: String(Math.floor(100000 + Math.random() * 900000)),
                        });
                      }
                      
                      // 检测学习资源是否修改
                      const resourceChanged =
                        (editData.videoContents !== undefined &&
                          JSON.stringify(editData.videoContents) !== JSON.stringify(selectedNode.videoContents)) ||
                        (editData.textContent !== undefined &&
                          JSON.stringify(editData.textContent) !== JSON.stringify(selectedNode.textContent));
                      
                      if (resourceChanged) {
                        newChanges.push({
                          type: 'modify',
                          target: selectedNode.name,
                          detail: '修改学习资源',
                          dimension: '学习资源',
                          changedByRole: isTeacher ? 'teacher' : 'supervisor',
                          changedByAccount: String(Math.floor(100000 + Math.random() * 900000)),
                        });
                      }
                      
                      // 将 editData 同步到 selectedNode（关键修复：确保数据保存）
                      if (infoChanged || structureChanged || resourceChanged || 
                          Object.keys(editData).length > 0) {
                        // 更新 selectedNode
                        const updatedNode: KnowledgeNode = {
                          ...selectedNode,
                          examFrequency: editData.examFrequency ?? selectedNode.examFrequency,
                          strategyType: editData.strategyType ?? selectedNode.strategyType,
                          personalizedStrategies: editData.personalizedStrategies ?? selectedNode.personalizedStrategies,
                          prerequisiteKnowledge: editData.prerequisiteKnowledge ?? selectedNode.prerequisiteKnowledge,
                          extensionKnowledge: editData.extensionKnowledge ?? selectedNode.extensionKnowledge,
                          videoContents: editData.videoContents ?? selectedNode.videoContents,
                          textContent: editData.textContent ?? selectedNode.textContent,
                        };
                        setSelectedNode(updatedNode);
                        
                        // 更新知识树中的对应节点
                        const updateNodeInTree = (nodes: KnowledgeNode[]): KnowledgeNode[] => {
                          return nodes.map(node => {
                            if (node.id === selectedNode.id) {
                              return updatedNode;
                            }
                            if (node.children) {
                              return { ...node, children: updateNodeInTree(node.children) };
                            }
                            return node;
                          });
                        };
                        setKnowledgeTree(prev => updateNodeInTree(prev));
                      }
                      
                      // 如果有变更，更新状态
                      if (newChanges.length > 0) {
                        setPendingChanges(prev => [...prev, ...newChanges]);
                        setPageState({
                          ...pageState,
                          status: 'pending',
                          pendingChanges: pageState.pendingChanges + newChanges.length,
                        });
                      }
                    }
                  }}
                  className="flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1.5 bg-emerald-600 text-white rounded-lg hover:bg-emerald-700 transition-colors text-sm font-medium"
                >
                  完成编辑
                </button>
                <button
                  onClick={() => setShowImportDialog(true)}
                  className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 border border-emerald-600 text-emerald-600 rounded-lg hover:bg-emerald-50 transition-colors text-sm font-medium"
                >
                  <Upload className="w-4 h-4" />
                  批量导入
                </button>
              </>
            ) : (
              <>
                <button
                  onClick={() => setIsEditingTree(true)}
                  className="flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1.5 border border-gray-300 text-gray-700 rounded-lg hover:bg-gray-50 transition-colors text-sm"
                >
                  <Edit className="w-4 h-4" />
                  编辑知识树
                </button>
                {/* 教研员身份下隐藏删除按钮 */}
                {!isTeacher && (
                  <button
                    onClick={() => setShowDeleteModal(true)}
                    className="flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1.5 border border-red-300 text-red-600 rounded-lg hover:bg-red-50 transition-colors text-sm"
                  >
                    <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                    删除
                  </button>
                )}
              </>
            )}
            <button className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 border border-gray-300 text-gray-700 rounded-lg hover:bg-gray-50 transition-colors text-sm font-medium">
              <Download className="w-4 h-4" />
              导出知识树
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 主体区域：左右布局 */}
      <div className="flex gap-4 h-[calc(100vh-200px)]">
        {/* 左侧：树形结构 */}
        <div className="w-80 bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm border border-gray-200 flex flex-col overflow-hidden">
          {/* 学段信息卡片 */}
          <div className="p-4 border-b border-gray-200">
            {/* 标题 */}
            <div className="mb-3">
              <span className="font-bold text-gray-900">知识树</span>
              <span className="px-2 py-0.5 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 rounded text-xs font-medium border border-emerald-200 ml-2">
                （{phaseConfig[selectedPhase].label}·{selectedSubject?.name}）
              </span>
            </div>
            {/* 详情区域 */}
            {pageState && (
              <div className="space-y-2 text-sm">
                {/* 版本行 */}
                <div className="flex items-center">
                  <span className="text-gray-600">版本：</span>
                  <span className="text-gray-900 font-mono ml-1">{pageState.currentVersion}</span>
                  <button
                    onClick={() => setShowHistoryModal(true)}
                    className="p-1 text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-100 rounded transition-colors ml-1"
                    title="查看历史版本"
                  >
                    <History className="w-4 h-4" />
                  </button>
                </div>
                {/* 状态行 */}
                <div className="flex items-center">
                  <span className="text-gray-600">状态：</span>
                  <span className="px-1.5 py-0.5 bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 rounded text-xs font-medium ml-1">已发布</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
          
          <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-2">
            <DndContext
              sensors={sensors}
              collisionDetection={closestCenter}
              onDragStart={({ active }) => setDragActiveId(active.id as string)}
              onDragEnd={handleTreeDragEnd}
              onDragCancel={() => setDragActiveId(null)}
            >
              <SortableContext
                items={getAllNodeIds(knowledgeTree)}
                strategy={verticalListSortingStrategy}
              >
                {knowledgeTree.length > 0 ? (
                  <>
                    {knowledgeTree.map(node => renderTreeNode(node))}
                    {/* 编辑模式下显示添加根知识点 - 教研员身份下不显示 */}
                    {isEditingTree && !isTeacher && (
                      <div className="mt-2">
                        {addingChildParentId === 'root' ? (
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 py-2 px-3">
                            <input
                              type="text"
                              value={addingChildValue}
                              onChange={(e) => setAddingChildValue(e.target.value)}
                              placeholder="请输入知识点名称"
                              className="flex-1 px-2 py-1 border border-emerald-300 rounded text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500"
                              autoFocus
                              onKeyDown={(e) => {
                                if (e.key === 'Enter' && addingChildValue.trim()) {
                                  handleAddNode(null, addingChildValue.trim());
                                }
                                if (e.key === 'Escape') {
                                  setAddingChildParentId(null);
                                  setAddingChildValue('');
                                }
                              }}
                            />
                            <button
                              onClick={() => {
                                if (addingChildValue.trim()) {
                                  handleAddNode(null, addingChildValue.trim());
                                }
                              }}
                              className="p-1 text-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-50 rounded"
                            >
                              <Check className="w-4 h-4" />
                            </button>
                            <button
                              onClick={() => {
                                setAddingChildParentId(null);
                                setAddingChildValue('');
                              }}
                              className="p-1 text-gray-400 hover:bg-gray-50 rounded"
                            >
                              <X className="w-4 h-4" />
                            </button>
                          </div>
                        ) : (
                          <button
                            onClick={() => setAddingChildParentId('root')}
                            className="w-full flex items-center gap-2 py-2 px-3 text-sm text-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-50 rounded-lg transition-colors"
                          >
                            <Plus className="w-4 h-4" />
                            添加根知识点
                          </button>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-48 text-gray-400">
                    <p className="text-sm">暂无知识点</p>
                    {/* 教研员身份下不显示立即添加按钮 */}
                    {isEditingTree && !isTeacher && (
                      <button
                        onClick={() => setAddingChildParentId('root')}
                        className="mt-2 text-emerald-600 text-sm hover:underline"
                      >
                        立即添加
                      </button>
                    )}
                  </div>
                )}
              </SortableContext>
            </DndContext>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* 右侧：详情编辑区 */}
        <div className="flex-1 bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm border border-gray-200 flex flex-col overflow-hidden">
          {selectedNode ? (
            <>
              {/* 标题区域 */}
              <div className="p-4 border-b border-gray-200">
                <h3 className="font-semibold text-gray-900 text-lg">{selectedNode.name}</h3>
              </div>
              
              {/* 根知识点引导界面 - 非编辑模式下显示 */}
              {isRootNode(selectedNode) && !isEditMode ? (
                <div className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center p-8">
                  <div className="text-center max-w-md">
                    {/* 图标 */}
                    <div className="w-24 h-24 bg-gradient-to-br from-emerald-100 to-emerald-50 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-6 shadow-inner">
                      <FolderTree className="w-12 h-12 text-emerald-600" />
                    </div>
                    
                    {/* 标题 */}
                    <h4 className="text-xl font-semibold text-gray-900 mb-3">根知识点</h4>
                    
                    {/* 说明文案 */}
                    <p className="text-gray-500 text-sm leading-relaxed mb-6">
                      根节点用于组织和分类下属知识点。可展开左侧树结构，选择具体的知识点查看详细信息。
                    </p>
                    
                    {/* 分隔线 */}
                    <div className="border-t border-gray-200 pt-6 mb-6">
                      {/* 统计信息 */}
                      <div className="bg-gray-50 rounded-xl p-5">
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
                          <span className="text-gray-500 text-sm">当前节点</span>
                          <span className="font-semibold text-gray-900">{selectedNode.name}</span>
                        </div>
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                          <span className="text-gray-500 text-sm">包含子知识点</span>
                          <span className="font-semibold text-emerald-600">{selectedNode.children?.length || 0} 个</span>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    
                    {/* 提示 */}
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-gray-400 text-xs">
                      <Info className="w-4 h-4" />
                      <span>点击左侧展开按钮查看下属知识点</span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <>
                  {/* Tab 切换栏 */}
                  <div className="border-b border-gray-200 px-4">
                    <div className="flex gap-6">
                      <button
                        onClick={() => setActiveDetailTab('info')}
                        className={`flex items-center gap-2 py-3 text-sm font-medium border-b-2 transition-colors ${
                          activeDetailTab === 'info'
                            ? 'text-emerald-600 border-emerald-600'
                            : 'text-gray-500 border-transparent hover:text-gray-700'
                        }`}
                      >
                        <FileText className="w-4 h-4" />
                        知识点信息
                      </button>
                      <button
                        onClick={() => setActiveDetailTab('structure')}
                        className={`flex items-center gap-2 py-3 text-sm font-medium border-b-2 transition-colors ${
                          activeDetailTab === 'structure'
                            ? 'text-emerald-600 border-emerald-600'
                            : 'text-gray-500 border-transparent hover:text-gray-700'
                        }`}
                      >
                        <BookOpen className="w-4 h-4" />
                        知识点结构
                      </button>
                      <button
                        onClick={() => setActiveDetailTab('resource')}
                        className={`flex items-center gap-2 py-3 text-sm font-medium border-b-2 transition-colors ${
                          activeDetailTab === 'resource'
                            ? 'text-emerald-600 border-emerald-600'
                            : 'text-gray-500 border-transparent hover:text-gray-700'
                        }`}
                      >
                        <Video className="w-4 h-4" />
                        学习资源
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6">
                {/* Tab: 知识点信息 */}
                {activeDetailTab === 'info' && (
                  <div className="space-y-6">
                    {/* 基本信息卡片 */}
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                      {/* 考频 - 仅叶子节点显示 */}
                      {isLeafNode(selectedNode) && (
                        <div className={`bg-gradient-to-br rounded-xl p-4 border relative overflow-hidden hover:shadow-md transition-shadow ${
                          isEditMode ? 'from-blue-50 to-white border-blue-300' : 'from-orange-50 to-white border-orange-200'
                        }`}>
                          <div className={`absolute top-0 right-0 w-20 h-20 rounded-full -mr-10 -mt-10 opacity-40 ${
                            isEditMode ? 'bg-blue-100' : 'bg-orange-100'
                          }`}></div>
                          <div className="relative">
                            <div className={`flex items-center gap-2 mb-2 ${
                              isEditMode ? 'text-blue-600' : 'text-orange-600'
                            }`}>
                              <Clock className="w-4 h-4" />
                              <span className={`text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide ${
                                isEditMode ? 'text-blue-600' : 'text-orange-600'
                              }`}>考频</span>
                            </div>
                            {isEditMode ? (
                              <select
                                value={editData.examFrequency ?? selectedNode.examFrequency}
                                onChange={(e) => setEditData({ ...editData, examFrequency: e.target.value as 'low' | 'medium' | 'high' })}
                                className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg text-base font-semibold focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 bg-white"
                              >
                                <option value="low">低频</option>
                                <option value="medium">中频</option>
                                <option value="high">高频</option>
                              </select>
                            ) : (
                              <div className={`text-2xl font-bold leading-none ${
                                selectedNode.examFrequency === 'high' ? 'text-red-600' :
                                selectedNode.examFrequency === 'medium' ? 'text-orange-600' :
                                'text-green-600'
                              }`}>
                                {selectedNode.examFrequency === 'high' ? '高频' :
                                 selectedNode.examFrequency === 'medium' ? '中频' : '低频'}
                              </div>
                            )}
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      )}

                      {/* 关联出题策略 - 仅叶子节点显示 */}
                      {isLeafNode(selectedNode) && (
                        <div className={`bg-gradient-to-br rounded-xl p-4 border relative overflow-hidden hover:shadow-md transition-shadow ${
                          (editData.strategyType ?? selectedNode.strategyType) === 'general'
                            ? 'from-emerald-50 to-white border-emerald-200'
                          : 'from-purple-50 to-white border-purple-200'
                      }`}>
                        <div className={`absolute top-0 right-0 w-20 h-20 rounded-full -mr-10 -mt-10 opacity-40 ${
                          (editData.strategyType ?? selectedNode.strategyType) === 'general' ? 'bg-emerald-100' : 'bg-purple-100'
                        }`}></div>
                        <div className="relative">
                          <div className={`flex items-center gap-2 mb-2 ${
                            (editData.strategyType ?? selectedNode.strategyType) === 'general' ? 'text-emerald-600' : 'text-purple-600'
                          }`}>
                            <Target className="w-4 h-4" />
                            <span className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide">选题策略</span>
                          </div>
                          {isEditMode ? (
                            <>
                              <select
                                value={editData.strategyType ?? selectedNode.strategyType}
                                onChange={(e) => setEditData({ ...editData, strategyType: e.target.value as 'general' | 'personalized' })}
                                className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500 bg-white"
                              >
                                <option value="general">通用策略</option>
                                <option value="personalized">个性化策略</option>
                              </select>
                              
                              {/* 个性化策略选择器 */}
                              {(editData.strategyType ?? selectedNode.strategyType) === 'personalized' && (
                                <div className="mt-3 space-y-3">
                                  {/* 模糊搜索选择器 */}
                                  <Popover open={showStrategySearch} onOpenChange={setShowStrategySearch}>
                                    <PopoverTrigger asChild>
                                      <button
                                        type="button"
                                        className={`flex-1 px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg text-sm text-left focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-purple-500 bg-white flex items-center justify-between ${
                                          !editData.personalizedStrategies?.[0] && !selectedNode.personalizedStrategies?.[0] ? 'text-gray-400' : 'text-gray-900'
                                        }`}
                                      >
                                        <span className="truncate">
                                          {editData.personalizedStrategies?.[0] || selectedNode.personalizedStrategies?.[0]
                                            ? personalizedStrategies.find(s => s.id === (editData.personalizedStrategies?.[0] || selectedNode.personalizedStrategies?.[0]))?.name
                                            : '请选择策略（支持模糊搜索）'}
                                        </span>
                                        <Search className="w-4 h-4 text-gray-400 flex-shrink-0 ml-2" />
                                      </button>
                                    </PopoverTrigger>
                                    <PopoverContent className="w-[320px] p-0" align="start">
                                      <Command>
                                        <CommandInput placeholder="搜索策略名称..." className="border-0" />
                                        <CommandList>
                                          <CommandEmpty>未找到匹配的策略</CommandEmpty>
                                          <CommandGroup>
                                            {personalizedStrategies.map((strategy) => (
                                              <CommandItem
                                                key={strategy.id}
                                                value={strategy.name}
                                                onSelect={() => {
                                                  setEditData({ ...editData, personalizedStrategies: [strategy.id] });
                                                  setShowStrategySearch(false);
                                                }}
                                                className="cursor-pointer"
                                              >
                                                <Check
                                                  className={`mr-2 h-4 w-4 flex-shrink-0 ${
                                                    (editData.personalizedStrategies?.[0] || selectedNode.personalizedStrategies?.[0]) === strategy.id
                                                      ? 'opacity-100'
                                                      : 'opacity-0'
                                                  }`}
                                                />
                                                <span className="text-sm font-medium flex-1">{strategy.name}</span>
                                              </CommandItem>
                                            ))}
                                          </CommandGroup>
                                        </CommandList>
                                      </Command>
                                    </PopoverContent>
                                  </Popover>
                                </div>
                              )}
                            </>
                          ) : (
                            <div className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-900 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                              <div className={`w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full ${
                                selectedNode?.strategyType === 'general' ? 'bg-emerald-600' : 'bg-purple-600'
                              }`}></div>
                              {selectedNode?.strategyType === 'general' ? '通用策略' : '同步学个性化策略（强化基础）'}
                            </div>
                          )}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>

                  {/* 策略详情面板 */}
                  {(editData.strategyType ?? selectedNode?.strategyType) === 'general' && (
                    <div className="mt-4 border border-gray-200 rounded-lg overflow-hidden">
                      <div className="bg-gray-50 px-4 py-3 border-b border-gray-200">
                        <h4 className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700 text-center">策略详情</h4>
                      </div>
                      <div className="overflow-x-auto">
                        <table className="w-full text-sm">
                          <thead>
                            <tr className="bg-gray-100 border-b border-gray-200">
                              <th className="px-4 py-3 text-left font-semibold text-gray-700 bg-white sticky left-0 border-r border-gray-200 min-w-[100px]">
                                策略维度
                              </th>
                              {mockStrategyDetails.map((detail, index) => (
                                <th key={index} className={`px-4 py-3 text-center font-semibold text-gray-700 ${
                                  index === 0 ? 'bg-red-50 border-l border-red-200' :
                                  index === 1 ? 'bg-orange-50 border-l border-orange-200' :
                                  index === 2 ? 'bg-yellow-50 border-l border-yellow-200' :
                                  'bg-green-50 border-l border-green-200'
                                }`}>
                                  <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2">
                                    <span className={`inline-flex items-center justify-center w-6 h-6 rounded-full text-xs font-bold ${
                                      index === 0 ? 'bg-red-500 text-white' :
                                      index === 1 ? 'bg-orange-500 text-white' :
                                      index === 2 ? 'bg-yellow-500 text-white' :
                                      'bg-green-500 text-white'
                                    }`}>
                                      {index + 1}
                                    </span>
                                    <span>{detail.range}</span>
                                  </div>
                                </th>
                              ))}
                            </tr>
                          </thead>
                          <tbody>
                            {/* 题目数量 */}
                            <tr className="border-b border-gray-100 hover:bg-gray-50">
                              <td className="px-4 py-3 font-medium text-gray-700 bg-white sticky left-0 border-r border-gray-200">
                                题目数量
                              </td>
                              {mockStrategyDetails.map((detail, index) => (
                                <td key={index} className={`px-4 py-3 text-center font-semibold text-gray-900 ${
                                  index === 0 ? 'bg-red-50/30' :
                                  index === 1 ? 'bg-orange-50/30' :
                                  index === 2 ? 'bg-yellow-50/30' :
                                  'bg-green-50/30'
                                }`}>
                                  {detail.questionCount}
                                  <span className="ml-1 text-xs font-normal text-gray-500">题</span>
                                </td>
                              ))}
                            </tr>

                            {/* 题目来源 */}
                            <tr className="border-b border-gray-100 hover:bg-gray-50">
                              <td className="px-4 py-3 font-medium text-gray-700 bg-white sticky left-0 border-r border-gray-200">
                                题目来源
                              </td>
                              {mockStrategyDetails.map((detail, index) => (
                                <td key={index} className={`px-4 py-4 text-center text-gray-800 leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-wrap ${
                                  index === 0 ? 'bg-red-50/30' :
                                  index === 1 ? 'bg-orange-50/30' :
                                  index === 2 ? 'bg-yellow-50/30' :
                                  'bg-green-50/30'
                                }`}>
                                  {detail.source}
                                </td>
                              ))}
                            </tr>

                            {/* 题目复杂度 */}
                            <tr className="border-b border-gray-100 hover:bg-gray-50">
                              <td className="px-4 py-3 font-medium text-gray-700 bg-white sticky left-0 border-r border-gray-200">
                                题目复杂度
                              </td>
                              {mockStrategyDetails.map((detail, index) => (
                                <td key={index} className={`px-4 py-4 text-center text-gray-800 leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-wrap ${
                                  index === 0 ? 'bg-red-50/30' :
                                  index === 1 ? 'bg-orange-50/30' :
                                  index === 2 ? 'bg-yellow-50/30' :
                                  'bg-green-50/30'
                                }`}>
                                  {detail.complexity}
                                </td>
                              ))}
                            </tr>

                            {/* 难度占比 */}
                            <tr className="border-b border-gray-100 hover:bg-gray-50">
                              <td className="px-4 py-3 font-medium text-gray-700 bg-white sticky left-0 border-r border-gray-200">
                                难度占比
                              </td>
                              {mockStrategyDetails.map((detail, index) => (
                                <td key={index} className={`px-4 py-4 text-center text-gray-800 leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-wrap ${
                                  index === 0 ? 'bg-red-50/30' :
                                  index === 1 ? 'bg-orange-50/30' :
                                  index === 2 ? 'bg-yellow-50/30' :
                                  'bg-green-50/30'
                                }`}>
                                  {detail.difficultyRatio}
                                </td>
                              ))}
                            </tr>

                            {/* 类型占比 */}
                            <tr className="border-b border-gray-100 hover:bg-gray-50">
                              <td className="px-4 py-3 font-medium text-gray-700 bg-white sticky left-0 border-r border-gray-200">
                                类型占比
                              </td>
                              {mockStrategyDetails.map((detail, index) => (
                                <td key={index} className={`px-4 py-4 text-center text-gray-800 leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-wrap ${
                                  index === 0 ? 'bg-red-50/30' :
                                  index === 1 ? 'bg-orange-50/30' :
                                  index === 2 ? 'bg-yellow-50/30' :
                                  'bg-green-50/30'
                                }`}>
                                  {detail.questionTypeRatio}
                                </td>
                              ))}
                            </tr>

                            {/* 年份范围 */}
                            <tr className="border-b border-gray-100 hover:bg-gray-50">
                              <td className="px-4 py-3 font-medium text-gray-700 bg-white sticky left-0 border-r border-gray-200">
                                年份范围
                              </td>
                              {mockStrategyDetails.map((detail, index) => (
                                <td key={index} className={`px-4 py-3 text-center text-gray-800 ${
                                  index === 0 ? 'bg-red-50/30' :
                                  index === 1 ? 'bg-orange-50/30' :
                                  index === 2 ? 'bg-yellow-50/30' :
                                  'bg-green-50/30'
                                }`}>
                                  {detail.yearRange}
                                </td>
                              ))}
                            </tr>

                            {/* 排序规则 */}
                            <tr className="border-b border-gray-100 hover:bg-gray-50">
                              <td className="px-4 py-3 font-medium text-gray-700 bg-white sticky left-0 border-r border-gray-200">
                                排序规则
                              </td>
                              {mockStrategyDetails.map((detail, index) => (
                                <td key={index} className={`px-4 py-3 text-center text-gray-800 ${
                                  index === 0 ? 'bg-red-50/30' :
                                  index === 1 ? 'bg-orange-50/30' :
                                  index === 2 ? 'bg-yellow-50/30' :
                                  'bg-green-50/30'
                                }`}>
                                  {detail.sortRule}
                                </td>
                              ))}
                            </tr>
                          </tbody>
                        </table>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  )}

                  {/* 个性化策略详情面板 */}
                  {(editData.strategyType ?? selectedNode?.strategyType) === 'personalized' && (editData.personalizedStrategies?.[0] || selectedNode?.personalizedStrategies?.[0]) && (
                    <div className="mt-4 border border-gray-200 rounded-lg overflow-hidden">
                      <div className="bg-gray-50 px-4 py-3 border-b border-gray-200">
                        <h4 className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700 text-center">策略详情</h4>
                      </div>
                      <div className="overflow-x-auto">
                        <table className="w-full text-sm">
                          <thead>
                            <tr className="bg-gray-100 border-b border-gray-200">
                              <th className="px-4 py-3 text-left font-semibold text-gray-700 bg-white sticky left-0 border-r border-gray-200 min-w-[100px]">
                                策略维度
                              </th>
                              {mockPersonalizedStrategyDetails.map((detail, index) => (
                                <th key={index} className={`px-4 py-3 text-center font-semibold text-gray-700 ${
                                  index === 0 ? 'bg-purple-50 border-l border-purple-200' :
                                  index === 1 ? 'bg-indigo-50 border-l border-indigo-200' :
                                  index === 2 ? 'bg-violet-50 border-l border-violet-200' :
                                  'bg-fuchsia-50 border-l border-fuchsia-200'
                                }`}>
                                  <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2">
                                    <span className={`inline-flex items-center justify-center w-6 h-6 rounded-full text-xs font-bold ${
                                      index === 0 ? 'bg-purple-500 text-white' :
                                      index === 1 ? 'bg-indigo-500 text-white' :
                                      index === 2 ? 'bg-violet-500 text-white' :
                                      'bg-fuchsia-500 text-white'
                                    }`}>
                                      {index + 1}
                                    </span>
                                    <span>{detail.range}</span>
                                  </div>
                                </th>
                              ))}
                            </tr>
                          </thead>
                          <tbody>
                            {/* 题目数量 */}
                            <tr className="border-b border-gray-100 hover:bg-gray-50">
                              <td className="px-4 py-3 font-medium text-gray-700 bg-white sticky left-0 border-r border-gray-200">
                                题目数量
                              </td>
                              {mockPersonalizedStrategyDetails.map((detail, index) => (
                                <td key={index} className={`px-4 py-3 text-center font-semibold text-gray-900 ${
                                  index === 0 ? 'bg-purple-50/30' :
                                  index === 1 ? 'bg-indigo-50/30' :
                                  index === 2 ? 'bg-violet-50/30' :
                                  'bg-fuchsia-50/30'
                                }`}>
                                  {detail.questionCount}
                                  <span className="ml-1 text-xs font-normal text-gray-500">题</span>
                                </td>
                              ))}
                            </tr>

                            {/* 题目来源 */}
                            <tr className="border-b border-gray-100 hover:bg-gray-50">
                              <td className="px-4 py-3 font-medium text-gray-700 bg-white sticky left-0 border-r border-gray-200">
                                题目来源
                              </td>
                              {mockPersonalizedStrategyDetails.map((detail, index) => (
                                <td key={index} className={`px-4 py-4 text-center text-gray-800 leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-wrap ${
                                  index === 0 ? 'bg-purple-50/30' :
                                  index === 1 ? 'bg-indigo-50/30' :
                                  index === 2 ? 'bg-violet-50/30' :
                                  'bg-fuchsia-50/30'
                                }`}>
                                  {detail.source}
                                </td>
                              ))}
                            </tr>

                            {/* 题目复杂度 */}
                            <tr className="border-b border-gray-100 hover:bg-gray-50">
                              <td className="px-4 py-3 font-medium text-gray-700 bg-white sticky left-0 border-r border-gray-200">
                                题目复杂度
                              </td>
                              {mockPersonalizedStrategyDetails.map((detail, index) => (
                                <td key={index} className={`px-4 py-4 text-center text-gray-800 leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-wrap ${
                                  index === 0 ? 'bg-purple-50/30' :
                                  index === 1 ? 'bg-indigo-50/30' :
                                  index === 2 ? 'bg-violet-50/30' :
                                  'bg-fuchsia-50/30'
                                }`}>
                                  {detail.complexity}
                                </td>
                              ))}
                            </tr>

                            {/* 难度占比 */}
                            <tr className="border-b border-gray-100 hover:bg-gray-50">
                              <td className="px-4 py-3 font-medium text-gray-700 bg-white sticky left-0 border-r border-gray-200">
                                难度占比
                              </td>
                              {mockPersonalizedStrategyDetails.map((detail, index) => (
                                <td key={index} className={`px-4 py-4 text-center text-gray-800 leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-wrap ${
                                  index === 0 ? 'bg-purple-50/30' :
                                  index === 1 ? 'bg-indigo-50/30' :
                                  index === 2 ? 'bg-violet-50/30' :
                                  'bg-fuchsia-50/30'
                                }`}>
                                  {detail.difficultyRatio}
                                </td>
                              ))}
                            </tr>

                            {/* 类型占比 */}
                            <tr className="border-b border-gray-100 hover:bg-gray-50">
                              <td className="px-4 py-3 font-medium text-gray-700 bg-white sticky left-0 border-r border-gray-200">
                                类型占比
                              </td>
                              {mockPersonalizedStrategyDetails.map((detail, index) => (
                                <td key={index} className={`px-4 py-4 text-center text-gray-800 leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-wrap ${
                                  index === 0 ? 'bg-purple-50/30' :
                                  index === 1 ? 'bg-indigo-50/30' :
                                  index === 2 ? 'bg-violet-50/30' :
                                  'bg-fuchsia-50/30'
                                }`}>
                                  {detail.questionTypeRatio}
                                </td>
                              ))}
                            </tr>

                            {/* 年份范围 */}
                            <tr className="border-b border-gray-100 hover:bg-gray-50">
                              <td className="px-4 py-3 font-medium text-gray-700 bg-white sticky left-0 border-r border-gray-200">
                                年份范围
                              </td>
                              {mockPersonalizedStrategyDetails.map((detail, index) => (
                                <td key={index} className={`px-4 py-3 text-center text-gray-800 ${
                                  index === 0 ? 'bg-purple-50/30' :
                                  index === 1 ? 'bg-indigo-50/30' :
                                  index === 2 ? 'bg-violet-50/30' :
                                  'bg-fuchsia-50/30'
                                }`}>
                                  {detail.yearRange}
                                </td>
                              ))}
                            </tr>

                            {/* 排序规则 */}
                            <tr className="border-b border-gray-100 hover:bg-gray-50">
                              <td className="px-4 py-3 font-medium text-gray-700 bg-white sticky left-0 border-r border-gray-200">
                                排序规则
                              </td>
                              {mockPersonalizedStrategyDetails.map((detail, index) => (
                                <td key={index} className={`px-4 py-3 text-center text-gray-800 ${
                                  index === 0 ? 'bg-purple-50/30' :
                                  index === 1 ? 'bg-indigo-50/30' :
                                  index === 2 ? 'bg-violet-50/30' :
                                  'bg-fuchsia-50/30'
                                }`}>
                                  {detail.sortRule}
                                </td>
                              ))}
                            </tr>
                          </tbody>
                        </table>
                      </div>
                      {/* 策略说明和适用场景 */}
                      <div className="border-t border-gray-200 bg-gray-50/50 p-4 space-y-3">
                        <div>
                          <label className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700">策略说明</label>
                          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-600 leading-relaxed">
                            针对基础薄弱的学生，重点强化基础知识。通过反复练习基础题目，帮助学生建立扎实的知识基础。根据学生错误率区间动态调整题目难度和数量，错误率越高，题目越基础；错误率越低，题目越综合。
                          </p>
                        </div>
                        <div>
                          <label className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700">适用场景</label>
                          <ul className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-600 space-y-1">
                            <li className="flex items-start gap-2">
                              <span className="text-purple-500 mt-0.5">•</span>
                              <span>学生基础测试得分低于60分</span>
                            </li>
                            <li className="flex items-start gap-2">
                              <span className="text-purple-500 mt-0.5">•</span>
                              <span>知识点掌握度评估为"未掌握"</span>
                            </li>
                            <li className="flex items-start gap-2">
                              <span className="text-purple-500 mt-0.5">•</span>
                              <span>需要针对性强化薄弱知识点</span>
                            </li>
                          </ul>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
                )}

                {/* Tab: 知识点结构 */}
                {activeDetailTab === 'structure' && (
                  <div className="space-y-6">
                    {/* 知识点关联关系 - 纵向流程布局 */}
                    <div className="bg-gradient-to-b from-gray-50 to-white rounded-xl border border-gray-200 p-6 hover:shadow-md transition-shadow">
                      {/* 标题 */}
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 mb-6 pb-3 border-b border-gray-200">
                        <div className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-500 to-orange-500"></div>
                        <label className="block text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700">
                          知识点关联关系
                        </label>
                        {isEditMode && (
                          <div className="flex gap-2 ml-auto">
                            <button
                              onClick={() => openKnowledgeSelector('prerequisite')}
                              className="text-xs text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 font-medium"
                            >
                              + 前置
                            </button>
                            <button
                              onClick={() => openKnowledgeSelector('extension')}
                              className="text-xs text-orange-600 hover:text-orange-800 font-medium"
                            >
                              + 延伸
                            </button>
                          </div>
                        )}
                      </div>

                      {/* 纵向流程 - 学习路径编辑器 */}
                      <div className="flex flex-col items-center">
                        {/* 前置知识点 - 按步骤分组展示 */}
                        {(() => {
                          const list = isEditMode ? editData.prerequisiteKnowledge : selectedNode?.prerequisiteKnowledge;
                          if (!list || list.length === 0) {
                            return (
                              <div className="w-full">
                                <div className="text-center text-sm text-gray-400 py-4 bg-blue-50/50 rounded-lg border border-dashed border-blue-200 min-w-[400px] max-w-[600px] mx-auto">
                                  暂无前置知识点，点击上方【+ 前置】添加
                                </div>
                              </div>
                            );
                          }
                          
                          // 按 stepId 分组
                          const stepMap = new Map<string, KnowledgeRelation[]>();
                          list.forEach((item, index) => {
                            const stepId = item.stepId || `step-${index}`;
                            if (!stepMap.has(stepId)) {
                              stepMap.set(stepId, []);
                            }
                            stepMap.get(stepId)!.push(item);
                          });
                          
                          const steps = Array.from(stepMap.entries()).sort((a, b) => {
                            const orderA = a[1][0]?.stepOrder ?? 0;
                            const orderB = b[1][0]?.stepOrder ?? 0;
                            return orderA - orderB;
                          });
                          
                          const totalSteps = steps.length;
                          
                          // 处理拖拽排序
                          const handlePrereqDragEnd = (event: { active: { id: string }; over: { id: string } | null }) => {
                            const { active, over } = event;
                            if (!over || active.id === over.id) return;
                            
                            const oldIndex = steps.findIndex(([id]) => id === active.id);
                            const newIndex = steps.findIndex(([id]) => id === over.id);
                            
                            if (oldIndex === -1 || newIndex === -1) return;
                            
                            // 重新排序步骤
                            const newSteps = arrayMove(steps, oldIndex, newIndex);
                            
                            // 更新每个知识点的 stepOrder
                            const newList = list.map(item => {
                              const stepIndex = newSteps.findIndex(([stepId]) => item.stepId === stepId);
                              if (stepIndex !== -1) {
                                return { ...item, stepOrder: stepIndex };
                              }
                              return item;
                            });
                            
                            setEditData({ ...editData, prerequisiteKnowledge: newList });
                          };
                          
                          return (
                            <DndContext
                              key="prereq-dnd"
                              onDragEnd={handlePrereqDragEnd as (event: { active: { id: unknown }; over: { id: unknown } | null }) => void}
                            >
                              <SortableContext items={steps.map(([id]) => id)}>
                                <div className="flex flex-col items-center w-full">
                                  {/* 顶部连接线起点 */}
                                  <div className="w-0.5 h-3 bg-gradient-to-b from-transparent to-blue-300"></div>
                                  
                                  {steps.map(([stepId, items], stepIndex) => (
                                    <DetailLayer
                                      key={stepId}
                                      stepId={stepId}
                                      stepIndex={stepIndex}
                                      items={items}
                                      totalSteps={totalSteps}
                                      type="prerequisite"
                                      isEditMode={isEditMode}
                                      onRemoveStep={() => {
                                        // 删除整层
                                        const newList = list.filter(item => item.stepId !== stepId);
                                        setEditData({
                                          ...editData,
                                          prerequisiteKnowledge: newList
                                        });
                                      }}
                                      onRemoveItem={(itemId) => {
                                        // 删除单个知识点
                                        setEditData({
                                          ...editData,
                                          prerequisiteKnowledge: list.filter(p => p.id !== itemId)
                                        });
                                      }}
                                      onMergeItemUp={(itemId) => {
                                        // 合并到上一层
                                        if (stepIndex === 0) return;
                                        const prevStepId = steps[stepIndex - 1][0];
                                        const newList = list.map(item => 
                                          item.id === itemId 
                                            ? { ...item, stepId: prevStepId, stepOrder: steps[stepIndex - 1][1][0]?.stepOrder ?? stepIndex - 1 }
                                            : item
                                        );
                                        setEditData({ ...editData, prerequisiteKnowledge: newList });
                                      }}
                                      onMergeItemDown={(itemId) => {
                                        // 合并到下一层
                                        if (stepIndex === totalSteps - 1) return;
                                        const nextStepId = steps[stepIndex + 1][0];
                                        const newList = list.map(item => 
                                          item.id === itemId 
                                            ? { ...item, stepId: nextStepId, stepOrder: steps[stepIndex + 1][1][0]?.stepOrder ?? stepIndex + 1 }
                                            : item
                                        );
                                        setEditData({ ...editData, prerequisiteKnowledge: newList });
                                      }}
                                      onSplitItemUp={(itemId) => {
                                        // 拆分为上一层
                                        if (stepIndex === 0) return;
                                        const newStepId = `step-${Date.now()}-split-up`;
                                        const newList = list.map(item => 
                                          item.id === itemId 
                                            ? { ...item, stepId: newStepId, stepOrder: stepIndex - 0.5 }
                                            : item
                                        );
                                        setEditData({ ...editData, prerequisiteKnowledge: newList });
                                      }}
                                      onSplitItemDown={(itemId) => {
                                        // 拆分为下一层
                                        if (stepIndex === totalSteps - 1) return;
                                        const newStepId = `step-${Date.now()}-split-down`;
                                        const newList = list.map(item => 
                                          item.id === itemId 
                                            ? { ...item, stepId: newStepId, stepOrder: stepIndex + 0.5 }
                                            : item
                                        );
                                        setEditData({ ...editData, prerequisiteKnowledge: newList });
                                      }}
                                      onSplitItemNew={(itemId) => {
                                        // 拆分为独立新层
                                        const newStepId = `step-${Date.now()}-split-new`;
                                        const newList = list.map(item => 
                                          item.id === itemId 
                                            ? { ...item, stepId: newStepId, stepOrder: stepIndex + 0.5 }
                                            : item
                                        );
                                        setEditData({ ...editData, prerequisiteKnowledge: newList });
                                      }}
                                      showConnector={stepIndex < steps.length - 1}
                                    />
                                  ))}
                                  
                                  {/* 连接线：前置 → 当前 */}
                                  <div className="flex flex-col items-center py-1">
                                    <div className="w-0.5 h-4 bg-gradient-to-b from-blue-200 to-purple-300"></div>
                                    <div className="w-3 h-3 text-purple-400">
                                      <ArrowDown className="w-3 h-3" />
                                    </div>
                                    <div className="w-0.5 h-4 bg-purple-300"></div>
                                  </div>
                                </div>
                              </SortableContext>
                            </DndContext>
                          );
                        })()}

                        {/* 当前知识点 - 居中高亮 */}
                        <div className="w-full flex justify-center">
                          <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-purple-500 to-indigo-500 rounded-xl px-6 py-2.5 shadow-lg min-w-[400px] max-w-[600px]">
                            <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-3">
                              <div className="flex-shrink-0">
                                <div className="w-10 h-10 bg-white rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-purple-600 shadow-md">
                                  <Target className="w-5 h-5" />
                                </div>
                              </div>
                              <div className="flex flex-col">
                                <span className="text-white/70 text-xs">当前知识点</span>
                                <span className="text-white font-semibold text-base">{selectedNode?.name}</span>
                              </div>
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                        
                        {/* 延伸知识点连接线 */}
                        {(() => {
                          const extList = isEditMode ? editData.extensionKnowledge : selectedNode?.extensionKnowledge;
                          return (extList && extList.length > 0) && (
                            <div className="flex flex-col items-center py-1">
                              <div className="w-0.5 h-4 bg-gradient-to-b from-purple-300 to-orange-200"></div>
                              <div className="w-3 h-3 text-orange-400">
                                <ArrowDown className="w-3 h-3" />
                              </div>
                              <div className="w-0.5 h-4 bg-orange-200"></div>
                            </div>
                          );
                        })()}

                        {/* 延伸知识点 - 按步骤分组展示 */}
                        {(() => {
                          const list = isEditMode ? editData.extensionKnowledge : selectedNode?.extensionKnowledge;
                          if (!list || list.length === 0) {
                            return (
                              <div className="w-full flex justify-center">
                                <div className="text-center text-sm text-gray-400 py-3 bg-orange-50/50 rounded-lg border border-dashed border-orange-200 min-w-[400px] max-w-[600px]">
                                  暂无延伸知识点，点击上方【+ 延伸】添加
                                </div>
                              </div>
                            );
                          }
                          
                          // 按 stepId 分组
                          const stepMap = new Map<string, KnowledgeRelation[]>();
                          list.forEach((item, index) => {
                            const stepId = item.stepId || `step-${index}`;
                            if (!stepMap.has(stepId)) {
                              stepMap.set(stepId, []);
                            }
                            stepMap.get(stepId)!.push(item);
                          });
                          
                          const steps = Array.from(stepMap.entries()).sort((a, b) => {
                            const orderA = a[1][0]?.stepOrder ?? 0;
                            const orderB = b[1][0]?.stepOrder ?? 0;
                            return orderA - orderB;
                          });
                          
                          // 计算起始序号（接在前置知识点后面）
                          const prereqList: KnowledgeRelation[] | undefined = isEditMode ? editData.prerequisiteKnowledge : selectedNode?.prerequisiteKnowledge;
                          const prereqStepCount = prereqList ? new Set(prereqList.map(p => p.stepId).filter((id): id is string => Boolean(id))).size : 0;
                          const startOrder = prereqStepCount + 1;
                          const totalSteps = steps.length;
                          
                          // 处理拖拽排序
                          const handleExtDragEnd = (event: { active: { id: string }; over: { id: string } | null }) => {
                            const { active, over } = event;
                            if (!over || active.id === over.id) return;
                            
                            const oldIndex = steps.findIndex(([id]) => id === active.id);
                            const newIndex = steps.findIndex(([id]) => id === over.id);
                            
                            if (oldIndex === -1 || newIndex === -1) return;
                            
                            // 重新排序步骤
                            const newSteps = arrayMove(steps, oldIndex, newIndex);
                            
                            // 更新每个知识点的 stepOrder
                            const newList = list.map(item => {
                              const stepIndex = newSteps.findIndex(([stepId]) => item.stepId === stepId);
                              if (stepIndex !== -1) {
                                return { ...item, stepOrder: stepIndex };
                              }
                              return item;
                            });
                            
                            setEditData({ ...editData, extensionKnowledge: newList });
                          };
                          
                          return (
                            <DndContext
                              key="ext-dnd"
                              onDragEnd={handleExtDragEnd as (event: { active: { id: unknown }; over: { id: unknown } | null }) => void}
                            >
                              <SortableContext items={steps.map(([id]) => id)}>
                                <div className="flex flex-col items-center w-full">
                                  {steps.map(([stepId, items], stepIndex) => (
                                    <DetailLayer
                                      key={stepId}
                                      stepId={stepId}
                                      stepIndex={startOrder + stepIndex}
                                      items={items}
                                      totalSteps={totalSteps}
                                      type="extension"
                                      isEditMode={isEditMode}
                                      onRemoveStep={() => {
                                        // 删除整层
                                        const newList = list.filter(item => item.stepId !== stepId);
                                        setEditData({
                                          ...editData,
                                          extensionKnowledge: newList
                                        });
                                      }}
                                      onRemoveItem={(itemId) => {
                                        // 删除单个知识点
                                        setEditData({
                                          ...editData,
                                          extensionKnowledge: list.filter(p => p.id !== itemId)
                                        });
                                      }}
                                      onMergeItemUp={(itemId) => {
                                        // 合并到上一层
                                        if (stepIndex === 0) return;
                                        const prevStepId = steps[stepIndex - 1][0];
                                        const newList = list.map(item => 
                                          item.id === itemId 
                                            ? { ...item, stepId: prevStepId, stepOrder: steps[stepIndex - 1][1][0]?.stepOrder ?? stepIndex - 1 }
                                            : item
                                        );
                                        setEditData({ ...editData, extensionKnowledge: newList });
                                      }}
                                      onMergeItemDown={(itemId) => {
                                        // 合并到下一层
                                        if (stepIndex === totalSteps - 1) return;
                                        const nextStepId = steps[stepIndex + 1][0];
                                        const newList = list.map(item => 
                                          item.id === itemId 
                                            ? { ...item, stepId: nextStepId, stepOrder: steps[stepIndex + 1][1][0]?.stepOrder ?? stepIndex + 1 }
                                            : item
                                        );
                                        setEditData({ ...editData, extensionKnowledge: newList });
                                      }}
                                      onSplitItemUp={(itemId) => {
                                        // 拆分为上一层
                                        if (stepIndex === 0) return;
                                        const newStepId = `step-${Date.now()}-split-up`;
                                        const newList = list.map(item => 
                                          item.id === itemId 
                                            ? { ...item, stepId: newStepId, stepOrder: stepIndex - 0.5 }
                                            : item
                                        );
                                        setEditData({ ...editData, extensionKnowledge: newList });
                                      }}
                                      onSplitItemDown={(itemId) => {
                                        // 拆分为下一层
                                        if (stepIndex === totalSteps - 1) return;
                                        const newStepId = `step-${Date.now()}-split-down`;
                                        const newList = list.map(item => 
                                          item.id === itemId 
                                            ? { ...item, stepId: newStepId, stepOrder: stepIndex + 0.5 }
                                            : item
                                        );
                                        setEditData({ ...editData, extensionKnowledge: newList });
                                      }}
                                      onSplitItemNew={(itemId) => {
                                        // 拆分为独立新层
                                        const newStepId = `step-${Date.now()}-split-new`;
                                        const newList = list.map(item => 
                                          item.id === itemId 
                                            ? { ...item, stepId: newStepId, stepOrder: stepIndex + 0.5 }
                                            : item
                                        );
                                        setEditData({ ...editData, extensionKnowledge: newList });
                                      }}
                                      showConnector={stepIndex < steps.length - 1}
                                    />
                                  ))}
                                </div>
                              </SortableContext>
