// 组件 Props 接口
interface TopicSystemProps {
  onDetailViewChange?: (isDetailView: boolean) => void;
}

export default function TopicSystem({ onDetailViewChange }: TopicSystemProps) {
  // 一级页面状态
  const [selectedPhase, setSelectedPhase] = useState<PhaseType>('senior');
  const [selectedSubject, setSelectedSubject] = useState('math');
  const [selectedGrade, setSelectedGrade] = useState('');
  const [selectedScene, setSelectedScene] = useState<SceneType | 'all'>('all');
  const [selectedTopic, setSelectedTopic] = useState<TopicCard | null>(null);

  // 二级页面状态
  const [isEditingTree, setIsEditingTree] = useState(false);
  const [topicTree, setTopicTree] = useState<TopicNode[]>(mockTopicTree);
  const [selectedNode, setSelectedNode] = useState<TopicNode | null>(null);
  const [hoveredNodeId, setHoveredNodeId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [searchKeyword, setSearchKeyword] = useState('');

  // 编辑状态
  const [editingNodeId, setEditingNodeId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [editingNodeValue, setEditingNodeValue] = useState('');
  const [addingParentId, setAddingParentId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [addingValue, setAddingValue] = useState('');
  const [showDeleteConfirm, setShowDeleteConfirm] = useState(false);
  const [deletingNodeId, setDeletingNodeId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // 弹窗状态
  const [showAddTopicDialog, setShowAddTopicDialog] = useState(false);
  const [newTopicName, setNewTopicName] = useState('');
  const [showKnowledgeSelector, setShowKnowledgeSelector] = useState(false);
  const [selectedKnowledgeIds, setSelectedKnowledgeIds] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const [knowledgeSearchKeyword, setKnowledgeSearchKeyword] = useState('');

  // 发布相关状态
  const [pageState, setPageState] = useState<TopicPageState | null>(null);
  const [showPublishModal, setShowPublishModal] = useState(false);
  const [showHistoryModal, setShowHistoryModal] = useState(false);
  const [pendingChanges, setPendingChanges] = useState<ChangeRecord[]>([]);
  const [publishDescription, setPublishDescription] = useState('');
  const [publishType, setPublishType] = useState<'immediate' | 'scheduled'>('immediate');
  const [scheduledDate, setScheduledDate] = useState('');
  const [scheduledTime, setScheduledTime] = useState('02:00');

  // 详情页 Tab
  const [activeDetailTab, setActiveDetailTab] = useState<DetailTabType>('info');

  // 监听详情页状态变化，通知父组件
  useEffect(() => {
    if (onDetailViewChange) {
      onDetailViewChange(!!selectedTopic);
    }
  }, [selectedTopic, onDetailViewChange]);

  // DnD sensors
  const sensors = useSensors(
    useSensor(PointerSensor),
    useSensor(KeyboardSensor, {
      coordinateGetter: sortableKeyboardCoordinates,
    })
  );

  // 加载页面状态
  useEffect(() => {
    if (selectedTopic) {
      const state = mockPageState[selectedTopic.id];
      if (state) {
        setPageState(state);
        if (state.pendingChanges > 0) {
          const changes: ChangeRecord[] = [
            { type: 'modify', target: '函数基础', detail: '修改知识点配置' },
            { type: 'add', target: '指数函数', detail: '新增知识点' },
          ];
          setPendingChanges(changes.slice(0, state.pendingChanges));
        } else {
          setPendingChanges([]);
        }
      }
    }
  }, [selectedTopic]);

  // 筛选专题卡片
  const filteredTopicCards = useMemo(() => {
    return mockTopicCards.filter(card => {
      const phaseMatch = !selectedPhase || card.phase === phaseConfig[selectedPhase].label;
      const subjectMatch = !selectedSubject || subjectConfig.find(s => s.id === selectedSubject)?.name === card.subject;
      const gradeMatch = !selectedGrade || gradeConfig[selectedPhase]?.find(g => g.id === selectedGrade)?.name === card.grade;
      const sceneMatch = selectedScene === 'all' || card.scene === selectedScene;
      return phaseMatch && subjectMatch && gradeMatch && sceneMatch;
    });
  }, [selectedPhase, selectedSubject, selectedGrade, selectedScene]);

  // ==================== 辅助函数 ====================

  // 生成节点编号
  const generateNodeNumber = (nodeId: string): string => {
    const parts = nodeId.split('-');
    if (parts.length === 1) {
      // 顶级节点，找到其在同级中的位置
      const index = topicTree.findIndex(n => n.id === nodeId);
      return `${index + 1}`;
    }
    // 子节点
    const parentId = parts[0];
    const parentIndex = topicTree.findIndex(n => n.id === parentId);
    const parent = topicTree[parentIndex];
    if (parent && parent.children) {
      const childIndex = parent.children.findIndex(c => c.id === nodeId);
      return `${parentIndex + 1}.${childIndex + 1}`;
    }
    return '';
  };

  // 检查节点是否有变更
  const hasNodeChanges = (node: TopicNode): boolean => {
    if (!pageState?.changedNodes) return false;
    if (pageState.changedNodes.includes(node.id)) return true;
    if (node.children) {
      for (const child of node.children) {
        if (hasNodeChanges(child)) return true;
      }
    }
    return false;
  };

  // 获取所有节点ID（用于拖拽）
  const getAllNodeIds = (nodes: TopicNode[]): string[] => {
    const ids: string[] = [];
    const traverse = (node: TopicNode) => {
      ids.push(node.id);
      if (node.children) {
        node.children.forEach(traverse);
      }
    };
    nodes.forEach(traverse);
    return ids;
  };

  // ==================== 编辑操作 ====================

  // 切换节点展开状态
  const toggleNode = (nodeId: string) => {
    const toggleRecursive = (nodes: TopicNode[]): TopicNode[] => {
      return nodes.map(node => {
        if (node.id === nodeId) {
          return { ...node, expanded: !node.expanded };
        }
        if (node.children) {
          return { ...node, children: toggleRecursive(node.children) };
        }
        return node;
      });
    };
    setTopicTree(toggleRecursive(topicTree));
  };

  // 编辑节点名称
  const handleEditNodeName = (nodeId: string, newName: string) => {
    const editRecursive = (nodes: TopicNode[]): TopicNode[] => {
      return nodes.map(node => {
        if (node.id === nodeId) {
          return { ...node, name: newName };
        }
        if (node.children) {
          return { ...node, children: editRecursive(node.children) };
        }
        return node;
      });
    };
    setTopicTree(editRecursive(topicTree));
    setEditingNodeId(null);
    setEditingNodeValue('');
  };

  // 添加大专题
  const handleAddTopic = (name: string) => {
    const newTopic: TopicNode = {
      id: `t${Date.now()}`,
      type: 'topic',
      name,
      order: topicTree.length + 1,
      expanded: true,
      children: [],
    };
    setTopicTree([...topicTree, newTopic]);
    setAddingParentId(null);
    setAddingValue('');
  };

  // 添加知识点引用
  const handleAddKnowledge = (parentId: string, knowledge: KnowledgeReference) => {
    const newKnowledge: TopicNode = {
      id: `k${Date.now()}`,
      type: 'knowledge',
      name: knowledge.name,
      order: 0,
      examCount: knowledge.examCount,
      knowledgeRef: knowledge,
    };
    
    const addRecursive = (nodes: TopicNode[]): TopicNode[] => {
      return nodes.map(node => {
        if (node.id === parentId) {
          const children = node.children || [];
          return { ...node, children: [...children, { ...newKnowledge, order: children.length + 1 }] };
        }
        if (node.children) {
          return { ...node, children: addRecursive(node.children) };
        }
        return node;
      });
    };
    setTopicTree(addRecursive(topicTree));
  };

  // 删除节点
  const handleDeleteNode = (nodeId: string) => {
    const deleteRecursive = (nodes: TopicNode[]): TopicNode[] => {
      return nodes.filter(node => {
        if (node.id === nodeId) return false;
        if (node.children) {
          node.children = deleteRecursive(node.children);
        }
        return true;
      });
    };
    setTopicTree(deleteRecursive(topicTree));
    if (selectedNode?.id === nodeId) {
      setSelectedNode(null);
    }
    setShowDeleteConfirm(false);
    setDeletingNodeId(null);
  };

  // 拖拽结束
  const handleDragEnd = (event: DragEndEvent) => {
    const { active, over } = event;
    if (over && active.id !== over.id) {
      // 简化处理：只处理同级排序
      // 实际项目中需要更复杂的逻辑处理跨级拖拽
    }
  };

  // 处理发布
  const handlePublish = (version: string, description: string, scheduledInfo?: { scheduledDate: string; scheduledTime: string }) => {
    if (!pageState) return;
    
    if (scheduledInfo) {
      // 定时发布：保存定时信息
      setPageState({
        ...pageState,
        status: 'pending',
        pendingChanges: 0,
        changedNodes: [],
        scheduledPublish: {
          version,
          scheduledDate: scheduledInfo.scheduledDate,
          scheduledTime: scheduledInfo.scheduledTime,
          description
        }
      });
      setPendingChanges([]);
    } else {
      // 立即发布
      const newRecord: PublishRecord = {
        version,
        description,
        publishTime: new Date().toLocaleString('zh-CN', {
          year: 'numeric',
          month: '2-digit',
          day: '2-digit',
          hour: '2-digit',
          minute: '2-digit',
        }).replace(/\//g, '-'),
        publisher: '当前用户',
      };

      setPageState({
        status: 'published',
        currentVersion: version,
        lastPublishTime: newRecord.publishTime,
        lastPublisher: newRecord.publisher,
        pendingChanges: 0,
        changedNodes: [],
        publishHistory: [newRecord, ...pageState.publishHistory],
      });
      setPendingChanges([]);
    }
  };

  // 取消定时发布
  const handleCancelScheduled = () => {
    if (!pageState || !pageState.scheduledPublish) return;
    
    // 恢复到待发布状态
    setPageState({
      ...pageState,
      status: 'pending',
      pendingChanges: 1,
      changedNodes: [],
      scheduledPublish: undefined
    });
    setPendingChanges([{ type: 'modify', target: '专题结构', detail: '取消定时发布' }]);
  };

  // 完成编辑
  const handleFinishEdit = () => {
    setIsEditingTree(false);
    if (pageState) {
      setPageState({
        ...pageState,
        status: 'pending',
        pendingChanges: pageState.pendingChanges + 1,
      });
      setPendingChanges(prev => [...prev, { type: 'modify', target: '专题结构', detail: '编辑专题内容' }]);
    }
  };

  // ==================== 子组件 ====================

  // 可排序树节点
  const SortableTreeNode = ({ id, isEditingTree }: { id: string; isEditingTree: boolean }) => {
    const { attributes, listeners, setNodeRef, transform, transition, isDragging } = useSortable({ id });

    const style = {
      transform: CSS.Transform.toString(transform),
      transition,
      opacity: isDragging ? 0.5 : 1,
    };

    return (
      <div ref={setNodeRef} style={style} {...attributes}>
        {/* 具体渲染在 renderTreeNode 中处理 */}
      </div>
    );
  };

  // 渲染树节点
  const renderTreeNode = (node: TopicNode, level: number = 0): React.ReactNode => {
    const isSelected = selectedNode?.id === node.id;
    const hasChildren = node.children && node.children.length > 0;
    const paddingLeft = isEditingTree ? level * 24 + 28 : level * 24 + 12;
    const nodeNumber = generateNodeNumber(node.id);
    const isHovered = hoveredNodeId === node.id;
    const isEditingThis = editingNodeId === node.id;
    const nodeHasChanges = hasNodeChanges(node);

    return (
      <div key={node.id} className="select-none">
        <div
          className={`group flex items-center gap-2 py-2 px-2 cursor-pointer transition-colors ${
            isSelected ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700' : 'text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-50'
          }`}
          style={{ paddingLeft: `${paddingLeft}px` }}
          onClick={() => !isEditingThis && setSelectedNode(node)}
          onMouseEnter={() => setHoveredNodeId(node.id)}
          onMouseLeave={() => setHoveredNodeId(null)}
        >
          {/* 编辑模式：拖拽手柄 */}
          {isEditingTree && (
            <div className="cursor-move touch-none flex-shrink-0">
              <GripVertical className="w-4 h-4 text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600" />
            </div>
          )}

          {/* 展开/收起按钮 */}
          {hasChildren ? (
            <button
              onClick={(e) => {
                e.stopPropagation();
                toggleNode(node.id);
              }}
              className="p-0.5 hover:bg-gray-200 rounded transition-colors"
            >
              {node.expanded ? (
                <ChevronDown className="w-4 h-4" />
              ) : (
                <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4" />
              )}
            </button>
          ) : (
            <span className="w-5 h-5" />
          )}

          {/* 自动编号 */}
          {isEditingTree && (
            <span className="text-xs text-gray-400 font-mono min-w-[20px]">{nodeNumber}</span>
          )}

          {/* 编辑模式：内联编辑 */}
          {isEditingThis ? (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-1">
              <input
                type="text"
                value={editingNodeValue}
                onChange={(e) => setEditingNodeValue(e.target.value)}
                className="flex-1 px-2 py-1 border border-emerald-300 rounded text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500"
                autoFocus
                onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
                onKeyDown={(e) => {
                  if (e.key === 'Enter' && editingNodeValue.trim()) {
                    handleEditNodeName(node.id, editingNodeValue.trim());
                  }
                  if (e.key === 'Escape') {
                    setEditingNodeId(null);
                    setEditingNodeValue('');
                  }
                }}
              />
              <button
                onClick={(e) => {
                  e.stopPropagation();
                  if (editingNodeValue.trim()) {
                    handleEditNodeName(node.id, editingNodeValue.trim());
                  }
                }}
                className="p-1 text-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-50 rounded"
              >
                <Check className="w-4 h-4" />
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={(e) => {
                  e.stopPropagation();
                  setEditingNodeId(null);
                  setEditingNodeValue('');
                }}
                className="p-1 text-gray-400 hover:bg-gray-50 rounded"
              >
                <X className="w-4 h-4" />
              </button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <>
              {/* 节点名称 */}
              <span className="flex-1 font-medium truncate">
                {node.type === 'topic' ? node.name : node.name}
              </span>

              {/* 有更新标签 */}
              {!isEditingTree && nodeHasChanges && pageState?.status === 'pending' && (
                <span className="text-xs px-1.5 py-0.5 bg-amber-100 text-amber-700 rounded font-medium whitespace-nowrap">
                  有更新
                </span>
              )}

              {/* 知识点：考试次数 */}
              {node.type === 'knowledge' && node.examCount !== undefined && (
                <span className="text-xs px-2 py-0.5 bg-orange-100 text-orange-600 rounded-full">
                  {node.examCount}次
                </span>
              )}

              {/* 知识点：引用标记 */}
              {node.type === 'knowledge' && (
                <span className="text-xs text-blue-500 flex items-center gap-0.5" title="引用自通用知识树">
                  <Link2 className="w-3 h-3" />
                  引用
                </span>
              )}

              {/* 编辑模式：操作按钮 */}
              {isEditingTree && isHovered && (
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
                  {/* 大专题：添加知识点 */}
                  {node.type === 'topic' && (
                    <button
                      onClick={(e) => {
                        e.stopPropagation();
                        setSelectedKnowledgeIds(new Set());
                        setKnowledgeSearchKeyword('');
                        setAddingParentId(node.id);
                        setShowKnowledgeSelector(true);
                      }}
                      className="p-1 text-gray-400 hover:text-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-50 rounded transition-colors"
                      title="添加知识点"
                    >
                      <Plus className="w-4 h-4" />
                    </button>
                  )}
                  {/* 编辑名称 */}
                  <button
                    onClick={(e) => {
                      e.stopPropagation();
                      setEditingNodeId(node.id);
                      setEditingNodeValue(node.name);
                    }}
                    className="p-1 text-gray-400 hover:text-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-50 rounded transition-colors"
                    title="编辑"
                  >
                    <Edit className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  </button>
                  {/* 删除 */}
                  <button
                    onClick={(e) => {
                      e.stopPropagation();
                      setDeletingNodeId(node.id);
                      setShowDeleteConfirm(true);
                    }}
                    className="p-1 text-gray-400 hover:text-red-600 hover:bg-red-50 rounded transition-colors"
                    title="删除"
                  >
                    <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  </button>
                </div>
              )}
            </>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* 子节点 */}
        {hasChildren && node.expanded && (
          <div>
            {node.children!.map(child => renderTreeNode(child, level + 1))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    );
  };

  // 状态条组件
  const StatusBar = () => {
    if (!pageState) return null;

    // 已发布状态：显示绿色提醒条
    if (pageState.status === 'published' && !pageState.scheduledPublish) {
      return (
        <div className="bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 rounded-lg px-4 py-3 mb-6">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Check className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-600" />
                <span className="text-sm text-emerald-800">
                  版本：<span className="font-medium">{pageState.currentVersion}</span>
                </span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm text-emerald-700">
                <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                <span>最近发布：{pageState.lastPublishTime}</span>
              </div>
              <span className="text-sm text-emerald-700">{pageState.lastPublisher}</span>
            </div>
            <button
              onClick={() => setShowHistoryModal(true)}
              className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 text-sm text-emerald-700 hover:text-emerald-800 hover:bg-emerald-100 rounded-lg transition-colors"
            >
              <History className="w-4 h-4" />
              查看历史版本
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      );
    }

    // 定时发布状态（无新变更）：显示蓝色提醒条
    if (pageState.scheduledPublish && pageState.pendingChanges === 0) {
      return (
        <div className="bg-blue-50 border border-blue-200 rounded-lg px-4 py-3 mb-6">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <Clock className="w-4 h-4 text-blue-600" />
              <span className="text-sm text-blue-800">
                <span className="font-medium">{pageState.scheduledPublish.version}</span> 版本将于您设定的时间（
                <span className="font-medium">{pageState.scheduledPublish.scheduledDate} {pageState.scheduledPublish.scheduledTime}</span>）自动发布，发布后更新的内容将正式生效
              </span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <button
                onClick={() => setShowHistoryModal(true)}
                className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 text-sm text-blue-700 hover:text-blue-800 hover:bg-blue-100 rounded-lg transition-colors"
              >
                <History className="w-4 h-4" />
                查看历史版本
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={handleCancelScheduled}
                className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 border border-blue-300 text-blue-700 rounded-lg hover:bg-blue-100 transition-colors text-sm"
              >
                取消定时
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      );
    }

    // 待发布状态（包括有定时信息但有新变更的情况）：显示橙色提醒条
    return (
      <>
        <div className="bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 rounded-lg px-4 py-3 mb-1">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <AlertTriangle className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-600" />
              <span className="text-sm text-amber-800">
                有 <span className="font-medium">{pageState.pendingChanges}</span> 项变更待发布，发布后才能生效
              </span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <button
                onClick={() => setShowHistoryModal(true)}
                className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 text-sm text-amber-700 hover:text-amber-800 hover:bg-amber-100 rounded-lg transition-colors"
              >
                <History className="w-4 h-4" />
                查看历史版本
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={() => {
                  // 如果有定时发布信息，回显数据
                  if (pageState.scheduledPublish) {
                    setPublishDescription(pageState.scheduledPublish.description);
                    setScheduledDate(pageState.scheduledPublish.scheduledDate);
                    setScheduledTime(pageState.scheduledPublish.scheduledTime);
                    setPublishType('scheduled');
                  }
                  setShowPublishModal(true);
                }}
                className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-1.5 bg-amber-600 text-white rounded-lg hover:bg-amber-700 transition-colors text-sm font-medium"
              >
                发布
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-start gap-2 text-xs text-gray-500 mb-6 pl-1">
          <Info className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400 mt-0.5 flex-shrink-0" />
          <span>信息发布将影响线上用户的实际使用，发布时，建议避开用户高频使用的时间段</span>
        </div>
      </>
    );
  };

  // 发布弹窗
  const PublishModal = () => {
    if (!showPublishModal || !pageState) return null;

    // 版本号：如果有定时发布信息，使用其版本号；否则生成新的
    const newVersion = pageState.scheduledPublish?.version || (() => {
      const currentParts = pageState.currentVersion.replace('v', '').split('.');
      return `v${currentParts[0]}.${parseInt(currentParts[1]) + 1}.0`;
    })();

    const handleConfirmPublish = () => {
      if (!publishDescription.trim()) return;
      
      if (publishType === 'scheduled') {
        // 定时发布：传递定时信息
        handlePublish(newVersion, publishDescription, {
          scheduledDate,
          scheduledTime
        });
      } else {
        // 立即发布
        handlePublish(newVersion, publishDescription);
      }
      
      setPublishDescription('');
      setPublishType('immediate');
      setScheduledDate('');
      setScheduledTime('02:00');
      setShowPublishModal(false);
    };

    return (
      <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black bg-opacity-50 flex items-center justify-center z-50">
        <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-xl w-[520px] max-h-[90vh] overflow-hidden">
          <div className="p-4 border-b border-gray-200 flex items-center justify-between">
            <h3 className="font-semibold text-gray-900">发布专题</h3>
            <button onClick={() => setShowPublishModal(false)} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600">
              <X className="w-5 h-5" />
            </button>
          </div>
          <div className="p-4 space-y-4">
            {/* 发布提示 */}
            <div className="bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 rounded-lg px-4 py-3">
              <div className="flex items-start gap-2">
                <AlertTriangle className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-600 mt-0.5 flex-shrink-0" />
                <div className="text-sm">
                  <p className="text-amber-800 font-medium">发布注意事项</p>
                  <p className="text-amber-700 mt-1">发布将影响线上用户的实际使用，建议避开用户高频使用的时间段。</p>
                  <p className="text-gray-500 mt-1 text-xs">建议发布时段：凌晨 00:00 - 04:00</p>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">版本号</label>
              <div className="px-3 py-2 bg-gray-100 border border-gray-200 rounded-lg text-gray-700 font-mono">
                {newVersion}
              </div>
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">
                更新说明 <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
              </label>
              <textarea
                value={publishDescription}
                onChange={(e) => setPublishDescription(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="请输入本次更新的内容说明..."
                className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500 resize-none"
                rows={3}
              />
            </div>

            {/* 发布时间 */}
            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-2">发布时间</label>
              <div className="space-y-2">
                <label className="flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer">
                  <input
                    type="radio"
                    name="publishType"
                    checked={publishType === 'immediate'}
                    onChange={() => setPublishType('immediate')}
                    className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-600"
                  />
                  <span className="text-sm text-gray-700">立即发布</span>
                </label>
                <label className="flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer">
                  <input
                    type="radio"
                    name="publishType"
                    checked={publishType === 'scheduled'}
                    onChange={() => setPublishType('scheduled')}
                    className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-600"
                  />
                  <span className="text-sm text-gray-700">定时发布</span>
                </label>
                {publishType === 'scheduled' && (
                  <div className="ml-6 flex items-center gap-3">
                    <input
                      type="date"
                      value={scheduledDate}
                      onChange={(e) => setScheduledDate(e.target.value)}
                      className="px-3 py-1.5 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500"
                    />
                    <input
                      type="time"
                      value={scheduledTime}
                      onChange={(e) => setScheduledTime(e.target.value)}
                      className="px-3 py-1.5 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500"
                    />
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>

            {pendingChanges.length > 0 && (
              <div>
                <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-2">变更记录</label>
                <div className="border border-gray-200 rounded-lg overflow-hidden">
                  {pendingChanges.map((change, idx) => (
                    <div key={idx} className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 border-b border-gray-100 last:border-0">
                      <span className={`px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs rounded ${
                        change.type === 'add' ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-700' :
                        change.type === 'modify' ? 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-700' :
                        'bg-red-100 text-red-700'
                      }`}>
                        {change.type === 'add' ? '新增' : change.type === 'modify' ? '修改' : '删除'}
                      </span>
                      <span className="text-sm text-gray-700">{change.target}</span>
                      <span className="text-xs text-gray-400">{change.detail}</span>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
          <div className="p-4 border-t border-gray-200 flex justify-end gap-2">
            <button
              onClick={() => setShowPublishModal(false)}
              className="px-4 py-2 text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-100 rounded-lg transition-colors"
            >
              取消
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={handleConfirmPublish}
              disabled={!publishDescription.trim()}
              className="px-4 py-2 bg-emerald-600 text-white rounded-lg hover:bg-emerald-700 transition-colors disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
            >
              {publishType === 'immediate' ? '确认发布' : '确认定时发布'}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  };

  // 历史版本弹窗
  const HistoryModal = () => {
    if (!showHistoryModal || !pageState) return null;

    return (
      <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black bg-opacity-50 flex items-center justify-center z-50">
        <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-xl w-[600px] max-h-[80vh] overflow-hidden">
          <div className="p-4 border-b border-gray-200 flex items-center justify-between">
            <h3 className="font-semibold text-gray-900">历史版本</h3>
            <button onClick={() => setShowHistoryModal(false)} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600">
              <X className="w-5 h-5" />
            </button>
          </div>
          <div className="p-4 max-h-[60vh] overflow-y-auto">
            {pageState.publishHistory.map((record, idx) => (
              <div
                key={idx}
                className={`p-3 border rounded-lg mb-2 ${
                  record.version === pageState.currentVersion
                    ? 'border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50'
                    : 'border-gray-200'
                }`}
              >
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
                  <span className="font-medium text-gray-900">{record.version}</span>
                  {record.version === pageState.currentVersion && (
                    <span className="text-xs px-2 py-0.5 bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 rounded-full">
                      当前版本
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>
                <p className="text-sm text-gray-600 mb-1">{record.description}</p>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-4 text-xs text-gray-400">
                  <span>{record.publishTime}</span>
                  <span>{record.publisher}</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  };

  // 知识点选择器弹窗
  const KnowledgeSelectorModal = () => {
    if (!showKnowledgeSelector) return null;

    const filteredKnowledge = mockKnowledgeTreeData.filter(kp =>
      kp.name.toLowerCase().includes(knowledgeSearchKeyword.toLowerCase())
    );

    return (
      <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black bg-opacity-50 flex items-center justify-center z-50">
        <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-xl w-[700px] max-h-[80vh] overflow-hidden">
          <div className="p-4 border-b border-gray-200 flex items-center justify-between">
            <div>
              <h3 className="font-semibold text-gray-900">选择知识点</h3>
              <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 mt-1">从通用知识树中选择知识点进行引用</p>
            </div>
            <button onClick={() => setShowKnowledgeSelector(false)} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600">
              <X className="w-5 h-5" />
            </button>
          </div>
          
          {/* 搜索 */}
          <div className="p-4 border-b border-gray-200">
            <div className="relative">
              <Search className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 transform -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 text-gray-400" />
              <input
                type="text"
                placeholder="搜索知识点..."
                value={knowledgeSearchKeyword}
                onChange={(e) => setKnowledgeSearchKeyword(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* 知识点列表 */}
          <div className="p-4 max-h-[50vh] overflow-y-auto">
            <div className="space-y-2">
              {filteredKnowledge.map(kp => (
                <label
                  key={kp.id}
                  className={`flex items-center gap-3 p-3 border rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors ${
                    selectedKnowledgeIds.has(kp.id)
                      ? 'border-emerald-300 bg-emerald-50'
                      : 'border-gray-200 hover:bg-gray-50'
                  }`}
                >
                  <input
                    type="checkbox"
                    checked={selectedKnowledgeIds.has(kp.id)}
                    onChange={(e) => {
                      const newSet = new Set(selectedKnowledgeIds);
                      if (e.target.checked) {
                        newSet.add(kp.id);
                      } else {
                        newSet.delete(kp.id);
                      }
                      setSelectedKnowledgeIds(newSet);
                    }}
                    className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-600 border-gray-300 rounded focus:ring-emerald-500"
                  />
                  <div className="flex-1">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <span className="font-medium text-gray-900">{kp.name}</span>
                      <span className="text-xs px-2 py-0.5 bg-orange-100 text-orange-600 rounded-full">
                        {kp.examCount}次
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mt-1 text-xs text-gray-500">
                      <span>{kp.sourcePhase} · {kp.sourceSubject}</span>
                      <span>·</span>
                      <span>{kp.strategyName}</span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </label>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* 底部按钮 */}
          <div className="p-4 border-t border-gray-200 flex justify-between items-center">
            <span className="text-sm text-gray-500">
              已选择 {selectedKnowledgeIds.size} 个知识点
            </span>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <button
                onClick={() => setShowKnowledgeSelector(false)}
                className="px-4 py-2 text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-100 rounded-lg transition-colors"
              >
                取消
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={() => {
                  // 添加选中的知识点
                  selectedKnowledgeIds.forEach(id => {
                    const kp = mockKnowledgeTreeData.find(k => k.id === id);
                    if (kp && addingParentId) {
                      handleAddKnowledge(addingParentId, kp);
                    }
                  });
                  setShowKnowledgeSelector(false);
                  setSelectedKnowledgeIds(new Set());
                }}
                disabled={selectedKnowledgeIds.size === 0}
                className="px-4 py-2 bg-emerald-600 text-white rounded-lg hover:bg-emerald-700 transition-colors disabled:bg-gray-300 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
              >
                确认添加
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  };

  // ==================== 页面组件 ====================

  // 一级页面：专题列表
  const TopicListPage = () => {
    // 根据场景筛选值决定显示哪些场景分组
    const displayScenes = selectedScene === 'all' 
      ? sceneOptions 
      : sceneOptions.filter(s => s.id === selectedScene);

    return (
      <div className="space-y-6">
        {/* 顶部筛选栏 */}
        <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm border border-gray-200 p-4">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-4 flex-wrap">
            {/* 学段 */}
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <label className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">学段：</label>
              <select
                value={selectedPhase}
                onChange={(e) => setSelectedPhase(e.target.value as PhaseType)}
                className="px-3 py-1.5 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500"
              >
                {Object.entries(phaseConfig).map(([key, config]) => (
                  <option key={key} value={key}>{config.label}</option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>

            {/* 学科 */}
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <label className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">学科：</label>
              <select
                value={selectedSubject}
                onChange={(e) => setSelectedSubject(e.target.value)}
                className="px-3 py-1.5 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500"
              >
                {subjectConfig.map((subject) => (
                  <option key={subject.id} value={subject.id}>{subject.name}</option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>

            {/* 年级 */}
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <label className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">年级：</label>
              <select
                value={selectedGrade}
                onChange={(e) => setSelectedGrade(e.target.value)}
                className="px-3 py-1.5 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500"
              >
                <option value="">全部年级</option>
                {gradeConfig[selectedPhase]?.map((grade) => (
                  <option key={grade.id} value={grade.id}>{grade.name}</option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>

            {/* 场景 */}
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <label className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">场景：</label>
              <select
                value={selectedScene}
                onChange={(e) => setSelectedScene(e.target.value as SceneType | 'all')}
                className="px-3 py-1.5 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500"
              >
                <option value="all">全部场景</option>
                {sceneOptions.map((scene) => (
                  <option key={scene.id} value={scene.id}>{scene.name}</option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* 按场景分组展示 */}
        {displayScenes.map((scene) => {
          // 筛选当前场景下的专题
          const sceneTopics = filteredTopicCards.filter(t => t.scene === scene.id);
          
          return (
            <div key={scene.id} className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm border border-gray-200 overflow-hidden">
              {/* 分组头部 */}
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-3 border-b border-gray-200 bg-gray-50">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                  <span className="text-xl">{scene.icon}</span>
                  <h3 className="font-semibold text-gray-900">{scene.name}</h3>
                  <span className="text-sm text-gray-500">（{sceneTopics.length}个专题）</span>
                </div>
                <button
                  onClick={() => {
                    setNewTopicName('');
                    setShowAddTopicDialog(true);
                  }}
                  className="flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1.5 text-sm text-emerald-600 hover:text-emerald-700 hover:bg-emerald-50 rounded-lg transition-colors"
                >
                  <Plus className="w-4 h-4" />
                  新增
                </button>
              </div>

              {/* 专题卡片列表 */}
              <div className="p-4">
                {sceneTopics.length > 0 ? (
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-4 xl:grid-cols-5 gap-4">
                    {sceneTopics.map((topic) => (
                      <div
                        key={topic.id}
                        onClick={() => setSelectedTopic(topic)}
                        className="group bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl overflow-hidden hover:shadow-lg hover:border-emerald-300 transition-all cursor-pointer"
                      >
                        {/* 封面 */}
                        <div className="aspect-[3/4] bg-gradient-to-br from-blue-100 to-blue-200 flex items-center justify-center relative">
                          <div className="text-center px-4">
                            <BookOpen className="w-12 h-12 text-blue-400 mx-auto mb-2" />
                            <span className="text-sm font-medium text-blue-600">{topic.name}</span>
                          </div>
                          {/* 发布状态标签 */}
                          <div className="absolute top-2 right-2">
                            {topic.publishStatus === 'pending' ? (
                              <span className="px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium text-amber-700 bg-amber-100 rounded-full">
                                待发布
                              </span>
                            ) : (
                              <span className="px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium text-emerald-700 bg-emerald-100 rounded-full">
                                已发布
                              </span>
                            )}
                          </div>
                        </div>
                        
                        {/* 信息 */}
                        <div className="p-3">
                          <h4 className="font-medium text-gray-900 truncate">{topic.name}</h4>
                          <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-1">
                            {topic.phase} | {topic.subject} | {topic.grade}
                          </p>
                          <div className="mt-2 pt-2 border-t border-gray-100 space-y-1">
                            <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
                              <span className="text-gray-500">📚 大专题</span>
                              <span className="font-medium text-gray-700">{topic.topicCount}</span>
                            </div>
                            <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
                              <span className="text-gray-500">📝 知识点</span>
                              <span className="font-medium text-gray-700">{topic.knowledgeCount}</span>
                            </div>
                          </div>
                          <div className="mt-2 pt-2 border-t border-gray-100">
                            <p className="text-xs text-gray-400">{topic.currentVersion}</p>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <div className="text-center py-12 text-gray-400">
                    <BookOpen className="w-12 h-12 mx-auto mb-3 opacity-50" />
                    <p>暂无专题，点击右上角"新增"创建</p>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    );
  };

  // 二级页面：专题详情
  const TopicDetailPage = () => {
    if (!selectedTopic) return null;

    return (
      <div className="space-y-4">
        {/* 状态条 */}
        <StatusBar />

        {/* 顶部导航 */}
        <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm border border-gray-200 p-4">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
              <button
                onClick={() => {
                  setSelectedTopic(null);
                  setSelectedNode(null);
                  setIsEditingTree(false);
                }}
                className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 text-gray-700 rounded-lg hover:bg-gray-50 transition-colors text-sm"
              >
                <ArrowLeft className="w-4 h-4" />
                返回
              </button>
              <div>
                <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-gray-900">{selectedTopic.name}</h2>
                <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">
                  {selectedTopic.phase} | {selectedTopic.subject} | {selectedTopic.grade} | 
                  {sceneOptions.find(s => s.id === selectedTopic.scene)?.name}
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              {isEditingTree ? (
                <>
                  <button
                    onClick={handleFinishEdit}
                    className="flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1.5 bg-emerald-600 text-white rounded-lg hover:bg-emerald-700 transition-colors text-sm font-medium"
                  >
                    完成编辑
                  </button>
                </>
              ) : (
                <button
                  onClick={() => setIsEditingTree(true)}
                  className="flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1.5 border border-gray-300 text-gray-700 rounded-lg hover:bg-gray-50 transition-colors text-sm"
                >
                  <Edit className="w-4 h-4" />
                  编辑专题
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* 主体区域：左右布局 */}
        <div className="flex gap-4 h-[calc(100vh-280px)]">
          {/* 左侧：专题树 */}
          <div className="w-80 bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm border border-gray-200 flex flex-col overflow-hidden">
            <div className="p-3 border-b border-gray-200">
              <h3 className="font-semibold text-gray-900 flex items-center gap-2">
                <Layers className="w-4 h-4" />
                专题结构
              </h3>
            </div>

            {/* 搜索框 */}
            <div className="p-3 border-b border-gray-200">
              <div className="relative">
                <Search className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 transform -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 text-gray-400" />
                <input
                  type="text"
                  placeholder="搜索知识点..."
                  value={searchKeyword}
                  onChange={(e) => setSearchKeyword(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* 树形结构 */}
            <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-2">
              <DndContext
                sensors={sensors}
                collisionDetection={closestCenter}
                onDragEnd={handleDragEnd}
              >
                <SortableContext
                  items={getAllNodeIds(topicTree)}
                  strategy={verticalListSortingStrategy}
                >
                  {topicTree.map(node => renderTreeNode(node))}
                </SortableContext>
              </DndContext>

              {/* 编辑模式：添加大专题 */}
              {isEditingTree && (
                <div className="mt-2 p-2">
                  {addingParentId === 'root' ? (
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        value={addingValue}
                        onChange={(e) => setAddingValue(e.target.value)}
                        placeholder="请输入大专题名称"
                        className="flex-1 px-2 py-1 border border-emerald-300 rounded text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500"
                        autoFocus
                        onKeyDown={(e) => {
                          if (e.key === 'Enter' && addingValue.trim()) {
                            handleAddTopic(addingValue.trim());
                          }
                          if (e.key === 'Escape') {
                            setAddingParentId(null);
                            setAddingValue('');
                          }
                        }}
                      />
                      <button
                        onClick={() => {
                          if (addingValue.trim()) {
                            handleAddTopic(addingValue.trim());
                          }
                        }}
                        className="p-1 text-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-50 rounded"
                      >
                        <Check className="w-4 h-4" />
                      </button>
                      <button
                        onClick={() => {
                          setAddingParentId(null);
                          setAddingValue('');
                        }}
                        className="p-1 text-gray-400 hover:bg-gray-50 rounded"
                      >
                        <X className="w-4 h-4" />
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    <button
                      onClick={() => setAddingParentId('root')}
                      className="w-full py-2 text-sm text-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-50 rounded-lg border border-dashed border-emerald-300 flex items-center justify-center gap-1"
                    >
                      <Plus className="w-4 h-4" />
                      添加大专题
                    </button>
                  )}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* 右侧：详情面板 */}
          <div className="flex-1 bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm border border-gray-200 flex flex-col overflow-hidden">
            {selectedNode ? (
              <>
                {/* Tab 切换 */}
                <div className="px-4 pt-4 border-b border-gray-200">
                  <div className="flex gap-4">
                    {[
                      { key: 'info', label: '知识点信息' },
                      { key: 'structure', label: '知识点结构' },
                      { key: 'resource', label: '学习资源' },
                    ].map((tab) => (
                      <button
                        key={tab.key}
                        onClick={() => setActiveDetailTab(tab.key as DetailTabType)}
                        className={`px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium border-b-2 transition-colors ${
                          activeDetailTab === tab.key
                            ? 'text-emerald-600 border-emerald-600'
                            : 'text-gray-500 border-transparent hover:text-gray-700'
                        }`}
                      >
                        {tab.label}
                      </button>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* 详情内容 - 只读 */}
                <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4">
                  {selectedNode.type === 'knowledge' && selectedNode.knowledgeRef ? (
                    <>
                      {/* 知识点信息 Tab */}
                      {activeDetailTab === 'info' && (
                        <div className="space-y-6">
                          {/* 引用提示 */}
                          <div className="bg-blue-50 border border-blue-200 rounded-lg p-4 flex items-start gap-3">
                            <Link2 className="w-5 h-5 text-blue-500 flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                            <div>
                              <h4 className="font-medium text-blue-800">引用自通用知识树</h4>
                              <p className="text-sm text-blue-600 mt-1">
                                来源：{selectedNode.knowledgeRef.sourcePhase} · {selectedNode.knowledgeRef.sourceSubject}
                              </p>
                              <p className="text-xs text-blue-500 mt-1">
                                此知识点信息为只读，如需编辑请前往【知识树】模块
                              </p>
                            </div>
                          </div>

                          {/* 基本信息 */}
                          <div className="border border-gray-200 rounded-lg overflow-hidden">
                            <div className="bg-gray-50 px-4 py-2 border-b border-gray-200">
                              <h4 className="font-medium text-gray-700">基本信息</h4>
                            </div>
                            <div className="p-4 space-y-3">
                              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                                <span className="text-gray-500">知识点名称</span>
                                <span className="font-medium text-gray-900">{selectedNode.knowledgeRef.name}</span>
                              </div>
                              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                                <span className="text-gray-500">考试次数</span>
                                <span className="font-medium text-gray-900">{selectedNode.knowledgeRef.examCount}次</span>
                              </div>
                              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                                <span className="text-gray-500">出题策略</span>
                                <span className={`px-2 py-1 text-xs rounded ${
                                  selectedNode.knowledgeRef.strategyType === 'general'
                                    ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700'
                                    : 'bg-purple-100 text-purple-700'
                                }`}>
                                  {selectedNode.knowledgeRef.strategyName}
                                </span>
                              </div>
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      )}

                      {/* 知识点结构 Tab */}
                      {activeDetailTab === 'structure' && (
                        <div className="space-y-4">
                          <div className="border border-gray-200 rounded-lg overflow-hidden">
                            <div className="bg-gray-50 px-4 py-2 border-b border-gray-200">
                              <h4 className="font-medium text-gray-700">前置知识</h4>
                            </div>
                            <div className="p-4">
                              {selectedNode.knowledgeRef.prerequisiteKnowledge?.length ? (
                                <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                                  {selectedNode.knowledgeRef.prerequisiteKnowledge.map(kp => (
                                    <span key={kp.id} className="px-3 py-1 bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 rounded-full text-sm">
                                      {kp.name}
                                    </span>
                                  ))}
                                </div>
                              ) : (
                                <p className="text-gray-400 text-sm">暂无前置知识</p>
                              )}
                            </div>
                          </div>

                          <div className="border border-gray-200 rounded-lg overflow-hidden">
                            <div className="bg-gray-50 px-4 py-2 border-b border-gray-200">
                              <h4 className="font-medium text-gray-700">延伸知识</h4>
                            </div>
                            <div className="p-4">
                              {selectedNode.knowledgeRef.extensionKnowledge?.length ? (
                                <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                                  {selectedNode.knowledgeRef.extensionKnowledge.map(kp => (
                                    <span key={kp.id} className="px-3 py-1 bg-purple-50 text-purple-700 rounded-full text-sm">
                                      {kp.name}
                                    </span>
                                  ))}
                                </div>
                              ) : (
                                <p className="text-gray-400 text-sm">暂无延伸知识</p>
                              )}
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      )}

                      {/* 学习资源 Tab */}
                      {activeDetailTab === 'resource' && (
                        <div className="text-center py-12 text-gray-400">
                          <BookOpen className="w-12 h-12 mx-auto mb-3 opacity-50" />
                          <p>学习资源功能开发中...</p>
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </>
                  ) : (
                    <div className="text-center py-12">
                      <Layers className="w-16 h-16 mx-auto mb-4 text-gray-300" />
                      <h3 className="text-lg font-medium text-gray-900 mb-2">{selectedNode.name}</h3>
                      <p className="text-gray-500">
                        {selectedNode.children?.length || 0} 个子节点
                      </p>
                      {isEditingTree && (
                        <button
                          onClick={() => {
                            setSelectedKnowledgeIds(new Set());
                            setKnowledgeSearchKeyword('');
                            setAddingParentId(selectedNode.id);
                            setShowKnowledgeSelector(true);
                          }}
                          className="mt-4 px-4 py-2 bg-emerald-600 text-white rounded-lg hover:bg-emerald-700 transition-colors text-sm"
                        >
                          添加知识点
                        </button>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              </>
            ) : (
              <div className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center text-gray-400">
                <div className="text-center">
                  <Eye className="w-12 h-12 mx-auto mb-3 opacity-50" />
                  <p>请选择左侧知识点查看详情</p>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  };

  // 新增专题弹窗
  const AddTopicDialog = () => {
    if (!showAddTopicDialog) return null;

    return (
      <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black bg-opacity-50 flex items-center justify-center z-50">
        <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-xl w-[500px] overflow-hidden">
          <div className="p-4 border-b border-gray-200 flex items-center justify-between">
            <h3 className="font-semibold text-gray-900">新增专题</h3>
            <button onClick={() => setShowAddTopicDialog(false)} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600">
              <X className="w-5 h-5" />
            </button>
          </div>
          <div className="p-4 space-y-4">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">
                专题名称 <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={newTopicName}
                onChange={(e) => setNewTopicName(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="请输入专题名称"
                className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500"
              />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">场景</label>
              <select
                value={selectedScene === 'all' ? '' : selectedScene}
                onChange={(e) => setSelectedScene(e.target.value as SceneType)}
                className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500"
              >
                {sceneOptions.map((scene) => (
                  <option key={scene.id} value={scene.id}>{scene.name}</option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">描述</label>
              <textarea
                placeholder="请输入专题描述"
                className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500 resize-none"
                rows={3}
              />
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="p-4 border-t border-gray-200 flex justify-end gap-2">
            <button
              onClick={() => setShowAddTopicDialog(false)}
              className="px-4 py-2 text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-100 rounded-lg transition-colors"
            >
              取消
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => {
                // TODO: 保存新专题
                setShowAddTopicDialog(false);
                setNewTopicName('');
              }}
              className="px-4 py-2 bg-emerald-600 text-white rounded-lg hover:bg-emerald-700 transition-colors"
            >
              确认创建
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  };

  // 删除确认弹窗
  const DeleteConfirmDialog = () => {
    if (!showDeleteConfirm) return null;

    return (
      <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black bg-opacity-50 flex items-center justify-center z-50">
        <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-xl w-[400px] overflow-hidden">
          <div className="p-4 border-b border-gray-200">
            <h3 className="font-semibold text-gray-900">确认删除</h3>
          </div>
          <div className="p-4">
            <p className="text-gray-600">确定要删除此节点吗？此操作不可撤销。</p>
          </div>
          <div className="p-4 border-t border-gray-200 flex justify-end gap-2">
            <button
              onClick={() => {
                setShowDeleteConfirm(false);
                setDeletingNodeId(null);
              }}
              className="px-4 py-2 text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-100 rounded-lg transition-colors"
            >
              取消
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => deletingNodeId && handleDeleteNode(deletingNodeId)}
              className="px-4 py-2 bg-red-600 text-white rounded-lg hover:bg-red-700 transition-colors"
            >
              确认删除
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  };

  // ==================== 渲染 ====================

  return (
    <div>
      {/* 一级页面 */}
      {!selectedTopic && <TopicListPage />}
      
      {/* 二级页面 */}
      {selectedTopic && <TopicDetailPage />}

      {/* 弹窗 */}
      <AddTopicDialog />
      <DeleteConfirmDialog />
      <KnowledgeSelectorModal />
      <PublishModal />
      <HistoryModal />
    </div>
  );
}

'use client';

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Save, Check } from 'lucide-react';
import PageLayout from '@/components/shared/PageLayout';

// 习题类型选项
const questionTypeOptions = [
  { id: 'single-choice', label: '单选题' },
  { id: 'multi-choice', label: '多选题' },
  { id: 'fill-blank', label: '填空题' },
  { id: 'judge', label: '判断题' },
  { id: 'cloze', label: '完型填空' },
  { id: 'reading', label: '阅读理解' },
  { id: 'qa', label: '问答题' },
  { id: 'composition', label: '作文' },
  { id: 'translation', label: '翻译题' },
  { id: 'listening', label: '听力题' },
  { id: 'material', label: '材料题' },
  { id: 'solution', label: '解答题' },
  { id: 'punctuation', label: '断句' },
  { id: 'modern-reading', label: '现代文阅读' },
  { id: 'classical-reading', label: '文言文阅读' },
  { id: 'poetry', label: '诗歌鉴赏' },
  { id: 'language-expression', label: '语言表达' },
  { id: 'calculation', label: '计算题' },
  { id: 'proof', label: '证明题' },
  { id: 'application', label: '应用题' },
  { id: 'written-expression', label: '书面表达' },
  { id: 'error-correction', label: '短文改错' },
  { id: 'fill-text', label: '短文填空' },
  { id: 'task-reading', label: '任务型阅读' },
  { id: 'word-fill', label: '选词填空' },
  { id: 'spelling', label: '单词拼写' },
  { id: 'drawing', label: '作图题' },
  { id: 'short-answer', label: '简答题' },
  { id: 'experiment', label: '实验探究题' },
  { id: 'analysis-calc', label: '分析计算题' },
  { id: 'sentence-transform', label: '句型转换' },
  { id: 'indefinite-choice', label: '不定项选择' },
  { id: 'oral', label: '口语题' },
  { id: 'handwriting', label: '手写题' },
  { id: 'photo', label: '拍照题' },
  { id: 'comprehensive', label: '综合题' },
];

// 学科配置
const subjectConfig = [
  { id: 'other', label: '其他' },
  { id: 'chinese', label: '语文' },
  { id: 'math', label: '数学' },
  { id: 'english', label: '英语' },
  { id: 'physics', label: '物理' },
  { id: 'chemistry', label: '化学' },
  { id: 'biology', label: '生物' },
  { id: 'politics', label: '政治' },
  { id: 'history', label: '历史' },
  { id: 'geography', label: '地理' },
];

// 各学科默认选中的题型
const defaultSelectedTypes: Record<string, string[]> = {
  'other': [],
  'chinese': ['single-choice', 'multi-choice', 'fill-blank', 'judge', 'qa', 'composition', 'translation', 'listening', 'material', 'punctuation', 'modern-reading', 'classical-reading', 'poetry', 'language-expression', 'spelling', 'oral', 'handwriting', 'comprehensive'],
  'math': ['single-choice', 'multi-choice', 'fill-blank', 'solution', 'calculation', 'proof', 'application', 'comprehensive'],
  'english': ['single-choice', 'multi-choice', 'fill-blank', 'cloze', 'reading', 'composition', 'listening', 'written-expression', 'error-correction', 'fill-text', 'task-reading', 'word-fill', 'spelling', 'sentence-transform', 'oral'],
  'physics': ['single-choice', 'multi-choice', 'fill-blank', 'solution', 'calculation', 'experiment', 'drawing'],
  'chemistry': ['single-choice', 'multi-choice', 'fill-blank', 'solution', 'calculation', 'experiment'],
  'biology': ['single-choice', 'multi-choice', 'fill-blank', 'solution', 'experiment'],
  'politics': ['single-choice', 'multi-choice', 'fill-blank', 'qa', 'short-answer'],
  'history': ['single-choice', 'multi-choice', 'fill-blank', 'qa', 'material', 'short-answer'],
  'geography': ['single-choice', 'multi-choice', 'fill-blank', 'qa', 'calculation', 'drawing'],
};

// 顶部标签配置
const tabsRow1 = [
  { id: 'exercise-type', label: '习题类型' },
  { id: 'exercise-config', label: '习题配置' },
  { id: 'exercise-tag', label: '习题标签' },
  { id: 'exam-tag', label: '试卷标签' },
  { id: 'publisher', label: '出版社设置' },
  { id: 'chapter', label: '教材章节设置', note: '后续隐藏' },
];

const tabsRow2 = [
  { id: 'knowledge-point', label: '知识点设置', note: '后续隐藏' },
  { id: 'resource-publisher', label: '资源库出版社管理' },
  { id: 'exercise-claim', label: '习题领取量设置' },
  { id: 'exercise-score', label: '习题分数设置' },
];

// 学科分组卡片组件
function SubjectCard({
  subjectId,
  subjectLabel,
  selectedTypes,
  onToggleType,
  onSelectAll,
  onClearAll,
  onSave,
}: {
  subjectId: string;
  subjectLabel: string;
  selectedTypes: string[];
  onToggleType: (typeId: string) => void;
  onSelectAll: () => void;
  onClearAll: () => void;
  onSave: () => void;
}) {
  const isAllSelected = selectedTypes.length === questionTypeOptions.length;
  const isNoneSelected = selectedTypes.length === 0;

  return (
    <div className="bg-white rounded-lg border border-gray-200 overflow-hidden">
      {/* 标题区 */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-5 py-3 bg-gray-50 border-b border-gray-200">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <span className="font-semibold text-gray-900">{subjectLabel}</span>
          <span className="text-sm text-gray-400">（已选 {selectedTypes.length}/{questionTypeOptions.length}）</span>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <button
            onClick={onSelectAll}
            disabled={isAllSelected}
            className={`text-sm ${isAllSelected ? 'text-gray-400 cursor-not-allowed' : 'text-emerald-600 hover:text-emerald-700'}`}
          >
            全选
          </button>
          <span className="text-gray-300">|</span>
          <button
            onClick={onClearAll}
            disabled={isNoneSelected}
            className={`text-sm ${isNoneSelected ? 'text-gray-400 cursor-not-allowed' : 'text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-700'}`}
          >
            清空
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={onSave}
            className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-1.5 bg-emerald-600 text-white rounded-lg hover:bg-emerald-700 transition-colors text-sm"
          >
            <Save className="w-4 h-4" />
            保存
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 题型选择区 */}
      <div className="p-5">
        <div className="grid grid-cols-6 lg:grid-cols-8 xl:grid-cols-9 gap-3">
          {questionTypeOptions.map((type) => {
            const isSelected = selectedTypes.includes(type.id);
            return (
              <label
                key={type.id}
                className={`
                  flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg border cursor-pointer transition-all text-sm
                  ${isSelected
                    ? 'bg-blue-50 border-blue-300 text-blue-700'
                    : 'bg-white border-gray-200 text-gray-600 hover:border-gray-300 hover:bg-gray-50'
                  }
                `}
              >
                <div
                  className={`
                    w-4 h-4 rounded border-2 flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0
                    ${isSelected
                      ? 'bg-blue-500 border-blue-500'
                      : 'border-gray-300 bg-white'
                    }
                  `}
                >
                  {isSelected && <Check className="w-3 h-3 text-white" />}
                </div>
                <span>{type.label}</span>
              </label>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function SystemSettingsPage() {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState('exercise-type');
  const [selectedTypesBySubject, setSelectedTypesBySubject] = useState<Record<string, string[]>>(defaultSelectedTypes);

  // 切换题型选中状态
  const toggleType = (subjectId: string, typeId: string) => {
    setSelectedTypesBySubject((prev) => {
      const current = prev[subjectId] || [];
      const newSelected = current.includes(typeId)
        ? current.filter((id) => id !== typeId)
        : [...current, typeId];
      return { ...prev, [subjectId]: newSelected };
    });
  };

  // 全选
  const selectAll = (subjectId: string) => {
    setSelectedTypesBySubject((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      [subjectId]: questionTypeOptions.map((t) => t.id),
    }));
  };

  // 清空
  const clearAll = (subjectId: string) => {
    setSelectedTypesBySubject((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      [subjectId]: [],
    }));
  };

  // 保存
  const handleSave = (subjectId: string) => {
    console.log(`保存 ${subjectId} 的配置:`, selectedTypesBySubject[subjectId]);
    // 模拟保存成功提示
    alert(`${subjectConfig.find(s => s.id === subjectId)?.label} 的习题类型配置已保存`);
  };

  return (
    <PageLayout
      activeMenuId="system-settings"
      breadcrumbs={[
        { label: '题库资源管理' },
        { label: tabsRow1.find(t => t.id === activeTab)?.label || '习题类型', isLast: true },
      ]}
    >
      <div className="flex flex-col h-full">
        {/* 标签栏 - 第一行 */}
        <div className="bg-white border-b border-gray-200 flex-shrink-0">
          <div className="px-6">
            <div className="flex items-end gap-8">
              {tabsRow1.map((tab) => (
                <button
                  key={tab.id}
                  onClick={() => setActiveTab(tab.id)}
                  className={`relative flex items-center gap-2 py-3 px-1 text-sm font-medium transition-colors ${
                    activeTab === tab.id
                      ? 'text-emerald-600'
                      : 'text-gray-600 hover:text-emerald-600'
                  }`}
                >
                  <span>{tab.label}</span>
                  {tab.note && (
                    <span className="text-xs text-amber-500 bg-amber-50 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded">
                      {tab.note}
                    </span>
                  )}
                  {activeTab === tab.id && (
                    <div className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 h-0.5 bg-emerald-600" />
                  )}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* 标签栏 - 第二行 */}
        <div className="bg-white border-b border-gray-200 flex-shrink-0">
          <div className="px-6">
            <div className="flex items-end gap-8">
              {tabsRow2.map((tab) => (
                <button
                  key={tab.id}
                  onClick={() => setActiveTab(tab.id)}
                  className={`relative flex items-center gap-2 py-3 px-1 text-sm font-medium transition-colors ${
                    activeTab === tab.id
                      ? 'text-emerald-600'
                      : 'text-gray-600 hover:text-emerald-600'
                  }`}
                >
                  <span>{tab.label}</span>
                  {tab.note && (
                    <span className="text-xs text-amber-500 bg-amber-50 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded">
                      {tab.note}
                    </span>
                  )}
                  {activeTab === tab.id && (
                    <div className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 h-0.5 bg-emerald-600" />
                  )}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* 主内容区 */}
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-auto bg-gray-50 p-6">
          {activeTab === 'exercise-type' ? (
            <div className="space-y-6 max-w-6xl">
              {subjectConfig.map((subject) => (
                <SubjectCard
                  key={subject.id}
                  subjectId={subject.id}
                  subjectLabel={subject.label}
                  selectedTypes={selectedTypesBySubject[subject.id] || []}
                  onToggleType={(typeId) => toggleType(subject.id, typeId)}
                  onSelectAll={() => selectAll(subject.id)}
                  onClearAll={() => clearAll(subject.id)}
                  onSave={() => handleSave(subject.id)}
                />
              ))}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="flex items-center justify-center h-full">
              <div className="text-center">
                <div className="w-24 h-24 mx-auto mb-6 bg-gray-100 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center">
                  <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-gray-200 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center text-gray-400 text-2xl">
                    🚧
                  </div>
                </div>
                <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold text-gray-900 mb-2">
                  {tabsRow1.find(t => t.id === activeTab)?.label || tabsRow2.find(t => t.id === activeTab)?.label}
                </h2>
                <p className="text-gray-500">该功能正在紧张开发中，敬请期待</p>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </PageLayout>
  );
}


=== FILE: src/app/system-settings/publisher-config/page.tsx ===
'use client';

import PageLayout from '@/components/shared/PageLayout';
import ComingSoon from '@/components/shared/ComingSoon';

export default function PublisherConfigPage() {
  return (
    <PageLayout
      activeMenuId="system-settings"
      breadcrumbs={[
        { label: '题库资源管理' },
        { label: '出版社设置', isLast: true },
      ]}
    >
      <ComingSoon 
        title="出版社设置" 
        description="该功能正在紧张开发中，敬请期待"
      />
    </PageLayout>
  );
}
